### [1] [A Kernel-Checked Exclusion Certificate for Erdős Problem 647](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17880)
*Ibrahim Mian,Shayaan Siddique*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文以 Lean 4 对 Erdős 问题 647 的有限区间版本进行了端到端形式化验证，证明不存在满足条件的整数 n（24<n≤10^9）。其核心贡献不在于扩大计算搜索范围，而在于将证明与证书重放完全置于可检查的形式化信任链内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有计算已将候选解排除至 10^12，或在模约化框架下达到约 9.17×10^18，但相关计算依赖内核外执行机制，不能构成完全由证明内核复核的结果。作者旨在提供首个不使用 native_decide、sorry 或问题专用公理的全程核验排除结果，并弥补此前一项已撤回论证中渐近步骤的缺口。

Method: 证明采用支配区间论证的有限形式：构造并在 Lean 4 中重放 6,685,922 个因子分解见证，由这些见证导出的排除区间恰好连续覆盖 (24,10^9]。证明仅需使用小于 1024 的素数相关事实。形式化开发的公理闭包严格为 {propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound}，不依赖 native_decide。生成流程还由两个独立实现交叉检查；编译后的开发通过 standalone lean4checker 重放，并在 gcc 与 clang 从源码构建的 Lean 工具链及无缓存重建的 mathlib 环境中复现，比较生成证书与 olean 摘要的一致性。

Result: Lean 4 已核验：不存在任何整数 n 满足 24<n≤10^9 且 max_{m<n}(m+τ(m))≤n+2。该结论通过全部 6,685,922 个证书见证的重放获得。作者报告两条从源码验证路径可逐字节复现提交的证书，且三个构建、两个架构上的 olean 摘要相同。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了 Erdős 问题 647 在区间 (24,10^9] 上首个完全端到端、可由形式化检查器复核的排除证明。虽然覆盖范围显著小于已有计算搜索前沿，但其价值在于显著缩小可信计算基并提供可独立复现的证明证书；它验证的是有限区间结论，而非该问题的完整否定或渐近性结论。

Abstract: Erdős problem 647 asks whether any $n > 24$ satisfies $\max_{m<n}(m + τ(m)) \le n + 2$, where $τ$ is the divisor-count function. Computational searches have excluded solutions up to $10^{12}$ by direct sieve and up to roughly $9.17 \times 10^{18}$ within a modular reduction whose Lean component relies on native_decide; those computations sit outside any proof kernel. We give the first exclusion checked end to end by one: no solution exists with $24 < n \le 10^9$, proved in Lean 4 with axiom closure exactly {propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound} -- no sorry, no native_decide, no problem-specific axiom. The proof replays a chain of 6,685,922 factorization witnesses whose excluded intervals concatenate across $(24, 10^9]$; it needs no primality facts beyond primes below 1024, and it is the finite, fully proved form of a domination-interval argument whose asymptotic step was the identified gap in a withdrawn January 2026 claim on this problem. The generation pipeline is cross-checked by two further independent implementations, the compiled development replays through the standalone lean4checker, and two from-source verification legs -- Lean toolchains compiled from source by gcc and by clang, mathlib rebuilt with no cache -- reproduce the committed certificates byte for byte, with olean digests identical across three builds on two architectures. Our range is three to ten orders of magnitude below the computational frontiers we cite; the contribution is the trust base, not the range.

</details>

### [2] [Graph Surgery and the Do-Operator: A Precise Correspondence for Acyclic Structural Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17634)
*Satpreet Makhija*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文形式化证明了确定性无环结构因果模型中，机制替换式干预与图上的删边手术在依赖关系层面严格一致，并分析了该一致性对含冗余边图模型的成立条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常将do算子的两种描述——图上删除目标节点入边，以及函数上用常数替换目标机制——称为等价，但二者输出对象不同：前者产生图结构，后者产生机制及施加值。因此需要建立精确的数学比较框架，说明机制层面的干预究竟如何对应图层面的依赖变化。

Method: 作者针对具有有限个内生变量的确定性无环结构因果模型，定义机制族F的依赖图Graph(F)，并将干预后的机制族记为F^ι、干预目标集合记为T_ι。核心证明建立Graph(F^ι)=Surg(Graph(F),T_ι)。此外，论文研究给定图G可能含有未被机制实际使用的冗余边时，何时可将Graph(F)替换为G；并进一步定义干预后的模型、刻画其运行结果、推导序列干预的组合规律，以及结果变量对实际依赖祖先处干预的依赖性质。

Result: 主要结果表明：以常数替换干预目标的结构方程，会且仅会移除图手术所移除的机制依赖边。若模型M=(G,F)中的G含有冗余边，则用G直接表达同一等式并不总是成立；该等式对所有干预均成立，当且仅当G恰好精确记录F的真实依赖关系。论文还证明，某个结果只依赖于其实际依赖祖先节点上的干预。

Conclusion: 该工作为do干预的图形语义与函数语义之间提供了严格的依赖层面桥梁：图删边并非仅是直观规则，而是机制替换在真实依赖结构上的精确投影。同时，论文强调因果图若包含未使用的箭头，可能破坏这种对应，因此精确依赖图是普遍保证干预图手术正确性的必要且充分条件。

Abstract: The $\operatorname{do}$-operator is described graphically by deleting arrows into its targets and functionally by replacing their mechanisms with constants. To call these operations equivalent is not yet a mathematical statement: one returns a graph and remembers only the targets, whereas the other returns mechanisms and also remembers the imposed values. We make a dependency-level comparison precise for deterministic acyclic structural causal models with finitely many endogenous variables. If $\operatorname{Graph}(F)$ extracts the dependencies of a mechanism family $F$, our main theorem is $\operatorname{Graph}(F^ι)=\operatorname{Surg}(\operatorname{Graph}(F),T_ι)$. Thus replacing target mechanisms removes exactly the dependencies removed by graph surgery. For a model $M=(G,F)$ whose graph may contain unused arrows, we characterize when the same equality holds with $G$ in place of $\operatorname{Graph}(F)$; it holds for every intervention exactly when $G$ records the dependencies of $F$ exactly. We then define the intervened model, characterize its run, show how sequential interventions combine, and prove that an outcome depends only on interventions at its actual dependency ancestors.

</details>


### [3] [GxP-Agent: Process-DAG Topology for Reliable Clinical Trial Programming with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16890)
*Jaime Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出GxP-Agent，一种面向临床试验编程的多智能体系统，通过将CDISC标准下的数据集生成流程编码为有向无环图（DAG），显著提升分析级数据集构建的可靠性与可验证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验编程需要把研究方案和原始数据转换为符合CDISC规范的分析级数据集，是申报流程中的关键瓶颈。现有大语言模型采用单次生成、单智能体或扁平多智能体方式时，难以处理严格的变量派生依赖、执行顺序和验证要求，导致生成结果无法满足实际使用标准。

Method: GxP-Agent将原本端到端的数据集生成任务拆分为15个领域专用节点，并以DAG显式约束节点间的先后依赖关系。各节点由具备pharmaverse技能上下文的工作智能体执行，系统同时引入验证门控和条件重试机制。作者构建CDISC-Bench执行型基准，以CDISCPilot01为基础评估ADSL生成能力，并进一步在ADAE数据集生成任务上检验泛化性。

Result: 在CDISC-Bench的ADSL任务中，采用Claude Sonnet 4.6的GxP-Agent在三次独立运行中均实现100%结构匹配，即49个变量全部正确且254条记录均正确。最佳检索增强基线仅达到59.2%，单智能体和扁平多智能体方法均为0%。DAG结构也提升了较弱模型的表现：GPT-4.1在该架构下平均结构匹配率为59.2%，而在其他架构下均为0%。在ADAE任务中，9节点分支DAG针对55个变量和1,191条记录首次尝试即达到100%结构匹配。

Conclusion: 论文表明，临床试验编程的可靠自动化不应主要依赖模型自身推理，而应将领域流程知识、数据派生依赖和质量控制规则显式编码为图拓扑与验证机制。DAG驱动的任务分解能够提高生成正确率、增强模型鲁棒性，并为符合GxP要求的临床数据编程自动化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Clinical trial programming -- transforming study protocols into analysis-ready datasets under CDISC standards -- is a bottleneck in regulatory submissions, yet LLM-based code generation fails catastrophically on this task: across 11 single-shot attempts with five frontier models, none produces a valid subject-level analysis dataset. We introduce GxP-Agent, a multi-agent system that encodes regulatory process ordering as a directed acyclic graph (DAG), decomposing monolithic dataset generation into 15 domain-specific nodes executed by worker agents with pharmaverse skill context, validation gates, and conditional retry. On CDISC-Bench, a new execution-based benchmark built from the FDA pilot submission CDISCPilot01 (254 subjects, 49 ground-truth ADSL variables), GxP-Agent with Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves 100% structural match (49/49 variables, 254 correct records) across three independent runs, compared to 59.2% for the best retrieval-augmented baseline and 0% for all single-agent and flat multi-agent approaches. The DAG topology also enables weaker models: GPT-4.1 achieves 59.2% mean structural match under the same DAG, where it scores 0% under every other architecture. The approach generalizes to ADAE (adverse events; 9-node branching DAG, 55 variables, 1,191 records), achieving 100% structural match on the first attempt. These results demonstrate that encoding domain process knowledge as graph topology -- rather than relying on LLM reasoning alone -- is a key enabler for reliable, GxP-compliant clinical trial programming.

</details>


### [4] [Adaptive Policy Portfolios for Robust Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17929)
*Kasper Engelen,Sebastian Junges,Guillermo A. Pérez,Marnix Suilen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程中的自适应策略组合：离线构造有限个无记忆随机策略，在线根据环境信息选择策略成员，以降低相对于环境最优策略的遗憾。核心贡献是刻画该组合的认证与合成问题的高计算复杂度，并提出可支持运行时专化的离线构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统鲁棒决策方法要求单一策略同时适应所有可能的转移函数，因此在真实但未知的环境动力学部署后逐渐可辨识时可能过度保守。策略组合允许预先准备多个候选策略，并在在线阶段利用观测信息进行选择，从而有望在保持鲁棒性的同时改善实际性能。论文采用鲁棒遗憾衡量组合质量，即在每个候选环境中，组合内最佳策略与该环境下事后最优策略之间的性能差距。

Method: 作者从复杂性理论角度分析两类问题：（1）给定策略组合的认证，即判断其鲁棒遗憾是否满足给定要求；（2）策略组合的合成，即寻找满足遗憾目标的有限策略集合。模型为鲁棒MDP，组合成员为无记忆随机策略，环境不确定性由转移函数集合或有理多面体表示。论文分别讨论确定性组合、一般有理多面体不确定集、固定折扣因子、无环动力学及单策略等受限情形，并给出一种适于运行时专化的离线组合构造方案。

Result: 给定组合的认证问题，即使在无环、状态—动作矩形不确定集的鲁棒MDP中且组合仅含确定性策略，也已达到∀R完全。对大小以一元编码有界的策略组合进行合成，在一般有理多面体不确定集下达到∃∀R完全；该结论即使在折扣因子固定且动力学无环时仍成立。论文还指出，组合大小为1的单策略特例同样同时具有组合复杂性与代数复杂性上的困难。

Conclusion: 策略组合能够缓解单一鲁棒策略的保守性，但其质量验证与自动合成在理论上都极其困难，属于实数计算复杂性层级中的高难问题。这表明精确的通用算法难以获得，实际系统更适合采用离线构造、近似求解或结构化限制，并在部署后通过轻量级在线选择器完成环境专化。

Abstract: Robust Markov decision processes optimize one policy against a set of plausible transition functions. This can be conservative when the unknown dynamics are fixed and become partially identifiable after deployment. We study adaptive policy portfolios: finite sets of memoryless randomized policies synthesized offline and paired with a lightweight online selector. Robust regret is a natural measure of portfolio quality: for each plausible environment, it measures the loss of the best portfolio member relative to the policy that would have been optimal had that environment been known. Related regret objectives were studied by Ghavamzadeh et al. (2016) with an emphasis on approximations and relaxations for safe policy improvement. We give a complexity-theoretic account of portfolio certification and synthesis. Certifying a given portfolio is $\forall\mathbb{R}$-complete already for deterministic portfolios in acyclic (s,a)-rectangular RMDPs. Synthesizing a portfolio of unary-bounded size is $\exists\forall\mathbb{R}$-complete for general rational polytopes, even with fixed discount and acyclic dynamics. The single-policy case is already hard, both combinatorially and algebraically. Finally, we present an offline portfolio construction that is amenable to runtime specialization.

</details>


### [5] [Runtime Governance for Agentic AI: Action-Boundary Control with Trusted Provenance and Fail-Closed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16891)
*Adam Mazzocchetti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Agentic AI systems request tool actions that can modify files, send messages, launch jobs, or change workflow state. This shifts the safety problem from harmful text generation to harmful operational side effects. Prompt-level governance can shape model behavior, but it does not create an execution boundary. We introduce Aegis, a runtime governance system that treats model outputs as action proposals and mediates them through a trusted decision layer before tool execution. The model proposes; the trusted runtime decides. Aegis evaluates proposals against active policy state, resolves provenance server-side, fails closed under uncertainty, and routes selected cases through Senate-style settlement, a quorum- based non-unilateral authorization path. We evaluate Aegis on a repeated sandbox corpus spanning five run families, 42 tasks, three conditions, and ten repeats per family. Across 6,300 rows, prompt-policy conditioning produced 79 risky comparator-path leakage rows. Across 2,100 Aegis-governed rows, the system recorded zero governed mock-tool applications and zero governed risky side-effect completions. All 1,832 Aegis-attempted governed rows preserved trusted Aegis-resolved provenance, and all 1,019 Senate-settled rows had quorum and final signed tally evidence. These results do not prove general autonomous-agent safety. They support the narrower systems claim that, in this evaluated sandbox corpus, runtime action-boundary governance prevented observed risky proposals from becoming governed side effects.

</details>


### [6] [The Price of Thinking: Reasoning Effort as a Model-Specific API Contract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16956)
*Yeabin Moon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究比较同一模型在“显式高推理强度”与“省略推理强度”两种API合同下的成本和准确率。显式高强度每次调用平均成本更高约0.01031美元，但未观察到统计上明确的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文强调API采购对象是包含模型版本、推理参数、输出限制、服务产品、提示词及定价规则在内的“日期化合同”，而非单一模型名称。研究旨在检验推理强度参数被省略时的实际语义，以及显式高强度是否带来可证实的性能收益。

Method: 作者采用预注册的配对对比设计，在30道AIME 2026题目上，对同一模型分别使用显式high推理强度和省略该参数的设置；每题各调用5次。每次付费调用被归入固定终止类别，统计推断以题目为重采样单位并保留题内重复调用。此外，研究使用日期化合同普查、Models-API元数据及预注册的原始响应探针考察参数省略的模型级语义。

Result: 显式高强度合同的平均交付成本较省略参数合同高0.01031美元/次，区间为[0.00204, 0.01974]。准确率差异为+0.0133，区间为[-0.0267, +0.0467]，因此未检测到明确的准确率差异，但设计无法排除最高4.67个百分点的增益。按注册口径的点估计，每道正确答案成本分别为：高强度0.08665美元、省略参数0.07662美元。对于省略参数的语义，证据显示其可能随模型而异，甚至同一服务商内部也不一致；当原始响应结构无法判定时，结论仅维持在文档证据等级。

Conclusion: 在所研究的模型、题目集和采集日期范围内，显式高推理强度带来了显著更高的调用成本，却没有提供可确认的准确率提升证据。因此，API使用者应将推理参数及其省略规则视为合同层面的关键变量，并基于具体模型、服务版本和日期进行独立评估，而不应假定省略参数具有统一或稳定的默认语义。

Abstract: API buyers purchase a dated contract, not a model name alone: the contract includes the requested and served model, reasoning-effort term or its omission, output rail, service product, prompt, and price schedule. We study the reasoning-effort term through a registered paired contrast of Sonnet 5 with explicit high effort against the same model with effort omitted, using 30 AIME 2026 items and five calls per item. Every paid attempt was assigned one frozen terminal category, and inference resampled items while retaining their repeated calls. Mean delivered cost was \$0.01031 per call higher under the explicit-high contract than under the omitted contract [+\$0.00204, +\$0.01974]. The corresponding accuracy contrast was +0.0133 [-0.0267, +0.0467]; we did not detect an accuracy difference, and the interval permits a gain of up to 4.67 percentage points that this design cannot rule out. Cost per correct answer was \$0.08665 under the high-effort contract and \$0.07662 under the omitted contract, as registered point estimates. A dated contract census, Models-API metadata, and preregistered raw-response probes further documented model-specific omission semantics, including within a provider; claims remained at documentation grade when raw structure was indeterminate. The request registry, parser, terminal taxonomy, statistical plan, and analysis pipeline were frozen before outcomes were examined; the resulting claims are bounded to the model, task, and collection date studied.

</details>


### [7] [FedPref: Federated Preference Learning for Structured Radiology Report Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16971)
*Flint Xiaofeng Fan,Cheston Tan,Yew-Soon Ong,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出FedPref，一种面向放射学报告结构化关系抽取的联邦偏好学习框架。该方法在不共享原始报告或标注的条件下，使数据量与标签分布不均的医疗机构协同训练模型，并显著改善低资源站点表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告中的发现及其解剖位置通常以自由文本形式存在，而检索和分析需要固定JSON模式。现实中，各机构的数据规模和疾病分布不均，小型机构缺少本地监督证据；同时，集中汇集医疗文本与标注往往不可行。因此，需要一种兼顾隐私保护、跨机构协作和弱资源机构收益的方法。

Method: FedPref使用冻结的公开语言模型生成多个候选JSON抽取结果，由各站点依据本地标注对候选结果进行偏好排序，形成偏好对。各机构据此训练紧凑的Qwen3-8B适配器，并仅通过联邦学习共享模型更新，不传输报告或人工标注。为缓解单一教师模型重复采样导致候选结果缺乏差异的问题，方法引入异构教师池，以提供跨模型对比样本。

Result: 在由6家模拟医院构成、且数据量和疾病患病率不均衡的开发集上，相比各站点独立训练，FedPref的客户端平均F1提高2.49个百分点，最差站点F1提高9.10个百分点；数据最少的站点获得最大收益。使用汇总偏好对进行集中训练的客户端平均F1比FedPref高2.66个百分点。在锁定的400份人工验证报告测试集上，FedPref达到68.68 F1，集中训练达到71.67 F1，二者排序与开发集一致。

Conclusion: FedPref证明了偏好学习与联邦训练可用于隐私受限的放射学结构化抽取任务。它虽仍落后于可访问全部偏好对的集中训练，但能够在完全不共享原始报告或标注的条件下有效提升跨机构性能，尤其有利于数据稀缺机构。

Abstract: Radiology reports describe findings and locations in free text, but downstream search and analysis require these relations in a fixed schema. Learning this extraction requires labels that are unevenly distributed across institutions: smaller hospitals have less local evidence, and pooling data may be infeasible. We introduce FedPref: frozen public language models propose alternative JSON extractions, local annotations rank them, and sites collaboratively train compact Qwen3-8B adapters while sharing only model updates. A heterogeneous teacher pool provides cross-model contrast when repeated single-model samples collapse. On development data from six simulated hospitals with unequal data volume and disease prevalence, FedPref improves client-mean F1 by 2.49 points and worst-site F1 by 9.10 points compared with training each site in isolation, with the largest gains at the sites holding the least data. Central training on the pooled preference-pair union is 2.66 points higher on client-mean F1. On a locked, 400-report manually validated gold test set, FedPref reaches 68.68 F1 and pooled training 71.67, preserving that same ordering. FedPref thus lets institutions with unequal, unpooled data benefit from collaboration without ever sharing reports or annotations.

</details>


### [8] [The Problem Is the Problem: Towards Scalable Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16977)
*Zeyu Zheng,Shengtong Zhang,Jeremy Avigad,Prasad Tetali,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 FAR（Find, Attempt, Recommend）人机协同发现框架：以研究方向而非预先指定的单一问题为输入，从大规模文献中自动挖掘开放问题、调用推理模型尝试求解，并通过多阶段筛选将有限专家审阅资源集中到高价值候选结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 辅助数学研究的主要瓶颈位于两端：其一是专家选择值得研究的问题，其二是专家审核大量机器生成的证明或结论。前沿模型推理预算与数学专家审阅能力均稀缺，因此需要一种能自动发现问题、评估问题状态并优先呈现高潜力结果的资源分配机制。

Method: FAR 构建了“文献检索—求解尝试—推荐审阅”的级联流程。系统首先依据研究方向扫描文献语料，抽取猜想和开放问题；随后过滤表述不完整、已解决或不适合处理的条目；再对保留问题进行模型推理与自动化分诊，依据候选解答的质量和潜在新颖性排序，最终将少量项目提交给作者团队或领域专家复核。组合数学试点以 5,245 篇论文为输入，形成从问题发现到人工复审的端到端管线。

Result: 试点共恢复 6,453 个候选猜想或开放问题，其中 4,717 个被判定为表述良好且似乎仍未解决。后续推理和自动分诊得到 598 个潜在解决方案，并筛选出 77 项进入作者团队审阅。审阅中发现了多项具有研究价值的结果，表明该系统不仅能够扩大问题搜索范围，也能显著压缩专家需要直接检查的候选数量。

Conclusion: 该工作将 AI-for-math 的交互单位从“人工指定单题”转变为“人工给定研究方向”，并用级联筛选管理推理与审阅资源。其核心贡献是提出可扩展的文献到审阅工作流，使专家更集中于高价值候选发现；不过，“仍然开放”的判定、自动推理结果的正确性及最终数学有效性仍需依赖严格的专家核验。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly capable of contributing to mathematical research. In research practice, frontier-model reasoning is a limited resource, and expert mathematical review is even more sharply constrained. Allocating these scarce resources well is therefore central to making AI-assisted mathematical discovery efficient. In most current AI-for-math workflows, human effort is concentrated at the beginning and end, in selecting suitable research problems and later reviewing the resulting artifacts. These two stages are becoming bottlenecks for research-level mathematics. We address them by proposing a new human-AI discovery paradigm. The human input is no longer a single problem selected in advance, but a research direction in which the experts have interest and expertise. The system then searches a broad literature corpus for candidate problems in that direction. Inspired by search and recommender systems, we build Find, Attempt, and Recommend (FAR), a literature-to-review cascade that automates the search for suitable problems and focuses human attention on artifacts that have passed several stages of filtering. In a combinatorics pilot, the pipeline starts from 5,245 combinatorics papers, recovers 6,453 candidate conjectures or open problems, and filters them to 4,717 apparently well-posed and still-open conjectures. Subsequent reasoning and automated triage stages surface 598 potential resolutions and select 77 items for author-team review. Among them, we identify many interesting discoveries, including results on conjectures and questions of Davies--Jenssen--Perkins--Roberts, Erdős--Straus, Ikenmeyer--Pak--Panova, and Lund--Saraf--Wolf. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of this new mode of human-AI collaboration for mathematical discovery.

</details>


### [9] [SkillEffect: Checked Lowering for Memory-Bounded Agent Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17007)
*Yinuo Wang,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SkillEffect：一种面向智能体工具调用的受检降级运行时，通过独立检查器、经审计的关系插件和有界执行机制，将高层技能约束转化为满足单次调用内存上限的具体程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型即使生成语义正确的工具程序，也可能因一次性载入完整输入而超出工具调用的固定内存预算。现有技能描述能够规定过程与资源义务，但缺少可验证、可复用且能在调度阶段强制执行内存约束的机制。

Method: SkillEffect要求每类计算提供一个经审计的关系插件，其中包括源识别器、输入事实提取器、有界中间表示构造器、arena内存上界函数和输出后置条件。独立检查器基于提交程序及不可变输入重建降级结果，确认其符合注册的资源关系；公共运行时负责受检选择、有界虚拟机执行、原子容量租赁和分阶段结果发布。系统的通用性来自共享的运行时与信任边界，而非自动支持任意计算；新计算仍需实现并审计对应插件。

Result: 在六类算子、五种执行模式上，有界访问降低了峰值内存，并在外部固定内存上限下提升了完成率。六个插件共享同一契约，覆盖流式归约和有界堆Top-k等模式。XLSX接入实验及Top-k扩展表明，新增数据关系或保留状态模式可复用既有信任边界；检查器接受全部评估中的合法配置，并拒绝全部对抗性提交方案。

Conclusion: SkillEffect证明了统一的受检降级架构能够在智能体工具调度时，对异构且预先注册的内存关系实施强制约束。其核心价值在于将程序正确性、资源上界验证和安全发布整合到共享运行时中，同时以插件审计换取可扩展性与可信性。

Abstract: Agent Skills can specify procedural and resource obligations for tool use, and language models instantiate them as concrete programs. However, when models turn this guidance into code for existing tool interfaces, even a semantically correct program may load an entire input and exceed the memory available to one tool call. We present SkillEffect, a checked-lowering runtime for computations with a recoverable source relation, an audited bounded implementation, and a registered output postcondition. Before granting execution authority, an independent checker rebuilds each proposed lowering from the submitted program and immutable input. Every relation plugin supplies a source recognizer, input-fact extractor, bounded-IR constructor, arena-bound function, and postcondition; one common runtime provides checked selection, bounded-VM execution, atomic capacity leasing, and staged publication. Generality in SkillEffect is architectural rather than automatic: each supported computation requires an audited relation plugin, while the dispatch, resource-control, execution, and publication mechanisms are shared across plugins. Across six operator families, bounded access substantially reduces peak memory and improves completion under externally fixed caps. Six plugins instantiate the same contract across five execution patterns, from streaming reduction to bounded-heap Top-k. The XLSX onboarding study and Top-k extension show that a new relation and a new retained-state pattern reuse the same trust boundary, while the checker accepts all evaluated legal configurations and rejects all adversarial proposals. Together, these results show that one checked-lowering architecture can enforce heterogeneous registered memory relations at Agent tool dispatch.

</details>


### [10] [Memory Is Communication: The Frontier Between Remembering and Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17053)
*Yashar Talebirad,Eden Redman,Ali Parsaee,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“记忆—信号前沿”框架，用于刻画资源受限智能体在保留历史信息与接收同伴消息之间的最优分配关系，并提出历史信息带来的任务损失降低越大，所需同伴通信越少的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限记忆与有限通信都会约束协作决策。历史记忆能够减少未来对通信的依赖，而同伴消息可补足记忆不足。现有问题在于：在固定任务和决策规则下，如何量化二者的替代关系，并识别达到给定性能水平所需的最低资源组合。

Method: 作者定义在指定历史使用规则和同伴观测规则下，达到特定性能阈值的记忆率—消息率组合构成的可达区域，并将其有效边界定义为“记忆—信号前沿”。随后提出可检验假设：若不同条件下历史信息均可达到相同的最大任务损失下降，则历史带来的实际损失下降越大，智能体所需的同伴通信越少。论文还以初步指称博弈观察作为支持，并建议通过系统操纵记忆率与消息率的实验估计该前沿。

Result: 初步实验表明，目标重复出现时，成功通信所使用的消息更短，说明可利用的历史记忆可能降低通信需求；但当目标仅能由隐藏循环规律预测时，消息长度未出现缩短。这意味着并非所有可预测性都会自动转化为更低的通信成本，信息能否被任务决策有效利用可能是关键条件。

Conclusion: 该工作将记忆和通信统一为可交换但并非完全等价的信息资源，并以记忆—信号前沿提供了量化其权衡的理论工具。其核心贡献是提出“历史信息的有效任务价值”而非单纯可预测性，可能决定通信节省程度。后续需在多种合作任务中通过受控实验验证该假设，并明确前沿形状受任务结构、决策规则及编码约束影响的机制。

Abstract: A bounded agent may obtain information for a decision from its own past, from peers, or from both sources. Retaining task-relevant history can reduce later communication, while a peer message can supply what memory lacks. Under limits on both resources, how should an agent allocate its information budget? Given a fixed task and decision rule, the memory and message rate pairs attaining a performance threshold form an achievable region under specified rules for using history and peer observations. We call its efficient boundary the remembering--signaling frontier. Across conditions where history permits the same maximum reduction in task loss, we hypothesize that a bounded agent will need less peer communication when it obtains a larger loss reduction from history. In preliminary referential games, target repetition coincided with shorter successful messages, while predictability from a hidden cyclic rule did not shorten them. Experiments varying memory and message rates can estimate the frontier and test this prediction across cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [11] [DiSCO: Defending text-to-image generation through distribution-guided contrastive prompt optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17067)
*Tong Zhang,Motasem Alfarra,Carlos Hinojosa,Christos Louizos,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Abstract: As text-to-image generative models advance, they raise critical safety concerns, particularly the generation of Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content such as violence and nudity, further exacerbated by red-teaming adversarial attacks. Existing defenses predominantly operate under white-box assumptions, relying on text encoder optimization, weight editing, or inference-time intervention, and fundamentally cannot scale to proprietary models. Black-box alternatives based on LLM prompt rewriting offer broader applicability, yet fail in a critical regime we identify as the \textit{benign adversarial} problem: prompts that are linguistically safe but still trigger harmful generation due to the model's learned data distribution. We propose DiSCO, a zero-shot, strictly black-box defense that operates entirely at the prompt level as a plug-and-play module, requiring no model retraining, fine-tuning, or access to model internals. DiSCO performs distribution-guided suffix expansion via beam search, optimized through contrastive scoring over safe and unsafe image pools generated by the target model itself, with iterative adaptive feedback until safe content is produced. We demonstrate that DiSCO consistently enhances the safety of both undefended and defended models on the I2P benchmark under multiple red-teaming attacks, achieving 37.7% and 25.13% ASR reduction, respectively, while maintaining semantic fidelity and improving image coherence. As a black-box, architecture-agnostic module, DiSCO can be readily applied to any text-to-image system without necessitating any changes to the model itself.

</details>
