Abstract: Background: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being applied to Software Engineering (SE) tasks, achieving high accuracy across problems such as clone detection, vulnerability prediction, and code summarization. However, their high computational demands and energy consumption raise sustainability concerns and hinder their use on consumer hardware and resource-constrained platforms. A common way to report the computational cost of an LLM in the literature and industry is to use the number of Floating Point Operations (FLOPs) required to perform a pass over the network. Aims: This paper investigates the implications of energy-aware knowledge distillation for SE, aiming to improve model efficiency while maintaining performance and to determine whether FLOPs is a reliable energy-aware metric. Method: We conduct a controlled experiment using Morph, a Many-Objective Optimization-based distillation methodology, to empirically examine whether FLOPs accurately reflect energy consumption in Clone Detection and Vulnerability Prediction tasks. We extend this methodology to include energy-surrogate models that directly estimate CPU and GPU energy consumption during optimization, and we apply Morph to generative tasks using CodeT5+ for code summarization. Results: Our results show that FLOPs is not always a reliable indicator of energy consumption, and better results can be achieved by using energy-surrogate models. Distilled student models can reduce inference energy consumption by up to 90\% and memory usage by 86\%, with only modest accuracy trade-offs. Conclusions: Energy-aware knowledge distillation when guided by direct energy surrogates rather than FLOPs can improve the energy consumption, sustainability, and deployability of LLMs for SE applications, enabling efficient models on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [77] [REST API Testing with Verified LLM-Inferred Dependencies and Response-Driven Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17546)
*Tu Nguyen,Thanh Nguyen,Huy Nguyen,Viet Nguyen,Tien N. Nguyen,Vu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Testing RESTful APIs requires generating sequences of API calls that satisfy dependencies among operations, parameters, and runtime-created resources. Recent LLM-based approaches infer such dependencies and generate test sequences from OpenAPI specifications, but they often treat LLM-inferred relationships as correct without execution-based validation. This can introduce spurious dependencies, miss feasible operation chains, and produce infeasible tests. In this paper, we propose APIPilot}, an execution-validated framework for REST API testing. APIPilot first derives candidate producer-consumer dependencies from OpenAPI specifications using structural heuristics and LLM-based semantic reasoning. It then treats these dependencies as hypotheses and validates them through concrete API executions before using them for test generation. The validated dependencies are organized into a dependency graph from which APIPilot constructs coverage-aware workflows via bounded top-k graph traversal, separating semantic dependency inference from sequence construction. To improve subsequent tests, APIPilot further performs response-driven refinement: runtime responses are analyzed to update resource pools, adjust input-generation constraints, and prune or revise invalid dependency mappings. Empirical evaluation on 16 real-world REST API services shows that APIPilot achieves 92.3% operation coverage, up to 58.6% code coverage, and an 88.1% workflow execution success rate, outperforming both LLM-based and traditional REST API testing baselines. APIPilot also detects 197 unique 5xx failures and specification-execution mismatches, demonstrating the benefit of grounding dependency inference in execution feedback.

</details>

Abstract: Modern embedded systems are becoming increasingly complex and typically integrate numerous heterogeneous devices, such as controllers, sensors, actuators, and supporting subsystems. As a result, their development and integration involve a wide variety of serial communication protocols, ranging from standardized solutions to partially standardized and fully project-defined formats. Efficient development of such systems increasingly depends on automation toolchains, which in turn require a clear, unified, and machine-processable formal basis. This paper proposes a unified, protocol-agnostic message model for explicit and deterministic description of serial messages. The model is based on formal definition of data types, atomic message elements (containers), and complete message structure. In addition to the model itself, the paper introduces methods for practical work with it, including configurable message types for expressing structural constraints and supporting deterministic automation, as well as configurable user representations for engineering-oriented reading and editing. The proposed model and methods are demonstrated through implementation in an industrial tool environment. The results show that the approach can support machine-readable interface control document development, automated generation of transport-layer software, and practical engineering work with both standardized and weakly formalized serial protocols. Taken together, the proposed model, methods, and tool implementation provide a practical foundation for automation toolchains in heterogeneous serial communication development.

</details>


### [79] [Benchmarking Automated Security Patch Backporting: How Far Are We?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17671)
*Jincheng Yang,Yulong Fu,Chengwei Liu,Lyuye Zhang,Fangyuan Zhang,Bingyang Ren,Yang Liu,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Porting Benchmark，系统评估安全补丁回移工具在跨版本、跨分支和跨仓库场景中的泛化能力，发现统一评测后工具表现差异明显，复杂补丁的成功率显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全补丁回移工具通常在单一仓库或特定项目版本上评估，报告的高成功率难以反映跨场景泛化能力。本文旨在建立统一、具有多样性的基准，比较不同技术路线并揭示实际修复中的关键瓶颈。

Method: 构建包含1,234个案例的Porting Benchmark，覆盖跨版本、跨分支和跨仓库任务，并在统一设置下评估五种基于程序分析、LLM提示和LLM智能体的工具。同时，对45个具备验证测试和构造POC的案例进行动态验证，比较参考实现匹配、可执行验证及可执行反馈优化的效果。

Result: 统一评测改变了工具性能排序：PortGPT和TSBPort在Replication Dataset上表现相对稳定，而FixMorph和Mystique性能明显下降。补丁结构复杂度升高时，最佳提交级成功率由Type-I的85.2%降至Type-IV的24.0%。主要失败原因包括目标API认知缺失、跨版本语义不匹配、非局部依赖传播失败，以及补丁构造或定位失败。参考实现匹配会低估困难目标适配的效果，并且无法发现部分目标环境中的集成失败；可执行反馈优化仅在最困难的可执行案例上带来有限但可测量的提升。

Conclusion: 安全补丁回移工具在同质化数据集上的高分不能代表真实场景中的泛化能力。未来研究应加强目标API与跨版本语义建模、非局部依赖处理、补丁定位和构造能力，并采用动态可执行验证作为静态匹配评估的重要补充。

Abstract: Automated security patch backporting is critical for mitigating N-day vulnerabilities. Recent tools report success rates above 80% on their respective datasets. However, these evaluations are often confined to homogeneous environments, such as one repository or specific project versions. Consequently, it remains unclear how well these tools generalize beyond their originally targeted scenarios. We present Porting Benchmark, a curated dataset of 1,234 security patch backporting cases spanning cross-version, cross-branch, and cross-repository scenarios, paired with a common evaluation framework. Using this benchmark, we evaluate five tools spanning program analysis, LLM prompting, and LLM agents under aligned settings. Our results show that aligned evaluation changes the apparent performance landscape: PortGPT and TSBPort remain comparatively strong on the Replication Dataset, while FixMorph and Mystique degrade substantially under the common protocol. Performance degrades sharply on structurally complex patches: the best commit-level success rate falls from 85.2% on Type-I patches to 24.0% on Type-IV. We identify four root-cause categories (missing target API awareness, cross-version semantic mismatch, non-local dependency propagation failure, and patch construction or localization failure) and derive concrete directions for next-generation tool design. On a 45-case dynamically validated subset with verified test cases and constructed POCs, we further observe that reference-based benchmark scores do not fully capture real-world remediation: exact match sharply under-credits harder target adaptations, while executable validation reveals residual integration failures in the target that static reference agreement misses. Executable-feedback refinement provides limited but measurable recovery on the hardest executable cases.

</details>


### [80] [GADR: Gathering Architecture Decision Records from Meeting Transcriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17694)
*Lucas Daniel Costa da Silva,Kiev Gama*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GADR，一种多智能体、自我纠错的工作流，用于从非结构化会议转录中提取架构决策并生成符合Nygard格式的架构决策记录（ADR）草稿。初步研究表明，该方法在决策捕获、结构遵循性和结果稳定性方面优于零样本与少样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的ADR生成方法通常假设输入已经具备良好结构，而真实架构决策往往产生于非正式、嘈杂且包含大量无关内容的会议。单轮提示难以处理隐含、分散和相互纠缠的决策信息，因此需要更稳健的自动化提取与文档生成流程。

Method: 构建GADR多智能体、自我纠错工作流，从原始会议转录中识别架构决策，并生成Nygard格式的ADR草稿。研究包含5份真实项目会议转录，由4名资深架构师进行专家评审，并通过15名学生开展补充评价；同时将GADR与零样本和少样本基线进行比较，并考察基于检索增强生成（RAG）的内容补充效果及其风险。

Result: GADR能够捕获大多数专家识别出的架构决策，生成的ADR草稿被参与者评价为清晰且有用。在稳定性和结构遵循性方面，GADR优于零样本和少样本基线。RAG能够提升ADR内容深度，但也可能引入与原始会议转录不一致的信息。

Conclusion: 研究初步验证了多智能体、自我纠错工作流在非结构化会议转录ADR生成中的可行性与潜力。然而，研究规模较小，且RAG引发的内容忠实性与可追溯性问题仍未解决。未来应重点加强决策来源追踪、事实一致性验证和更大规模的实证评估。

Abstract: Existing LLM-based approaches to Architecture Decision Record (ADR) generation share a critical and largely unexamined assumption: that input is already reasonably structured. In practice, architectural decisions emerge from informal, noisy meetings where choices are implicit, fragmented, and entangled with off-topic dialogue, precisely the conditions under which single-pass prompting degrades. This paper presents GADR, a multi-agent, self-correcting workflow that extracts architectural decisions from raw meeting transcriptions and generates Nygard-formatted ADR drafts. A feasibility study comprising five real project meeting transcripts, expert review by four senior architects, and evaluation by fifteen students provides initial evidence that the agentic workflow captures most expert-identified decisions and produces drafts participants found clear and useful, outperforming zero-shot and few-shot baselines in stability and structural adherence. The study also addresses the underexplored trade-off of RAG-based enrichment improving ADR depth while simultaneously risking transcript-unfaithful content, raising open questions about traceability in automated architectural documentation that we believe is worth the community's attention.

</details>


### [81] [What Aggregate Scores Miss: Measuring Item-Level Regressions in Commercial LLM API Migrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17719)
*Xiaonan Xu,Wenjing Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究表明，使用汇总基准分数评估商业大语言模型升级会掩盖显著的题目级双向变化：模型整体得分提升时仍可能有部分题目可靠退化，整体下降时也可能有部分题目可靠改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业大语言模型供应商弃用旧版本后，软件系统必须迁移至后继模型。现有迁移决策主要依赖汇总基准分数，但单一总分可能掩盖不同题目的异质性表现，因此有必要系统评估这种信息压缩带来的影响。

Method: 研究考察GPT-5.4至GPT-5.6 Sol产品序列中的三组模型升级，在研究生知识、竞赛数学和指令遵循三个公开基准上测试900个题目。每个题目由每个模型重复回答50次，并在错误发现率控制和实际显著性阈值下，将题目划分为可靠改善、可靠退化、实际等价或结果不确定；同时使用标签置换零假设进行校准。

Result: 九个迁移—基准组合均同时出现可靠改善和可靠退化。总体得分最高提升7.3个百分点的模型升级中，最多有8.3%的题目可靠退化；总体得分下降的升级中，最多有10.7%的题目可靠改善。在最新迁移的指令遵循基准上，严格评分与宽松评分的差异扩大3.9个百分点：严格评分显示3.9个百分点的退化，而宽松评分下退化幅度仅为0.04个百分点。研究还发布了完整响应档案和逐题评分结果。

Conclusion: 仅依据汇总基准分数进行模型迁移决策是不充分的，因为它无法揭示重要的题目级双向变化。更可靠的迁移评估应结合逐题分析、重复测量、统计显著性控制、实际显著性阈值以及不同评分标准的敏感性分析。

Abstract: Context: Software systems that depend on commercial large language model APIs must migrate to successor versions when vendors deprecate older models. Migration decisions typically rely on aggregate benchmark scores, which compress heterogeneous item-level behaviour into a single net figure. Objective: We measure what that compression conceals. Method: On three pairwise upgrades in the GPT-5.4 to GPT-5.6 Sol product sequence, we query 900 public benchmark items (graduate-level knowledge, olympiad mathematics, instruction following) 50 times per item per model, classify each item as reliably improved, reliably regressed, practically equivalent, or inconclusive under false-discovery-rate control and a practical-significance threshold, and calibrate the results against a label-permutation null. Results: Across all nine migration-benchmark cells, reliable improvements and reliable regressions coexist. Edges with aggregate gains of up to 7.3 percentage points contain up to 8.3% reliably regressed items; edges with aggregate losses contain up to 10.7% reliably improved items. On the instruction-following benchmark, the gap between strict and loose scoring widens by 3.9 percentage points on the latest migration: a 3.9-point regression under strict scoring shrinks to 0.04 points under loose scoring. Conclusion: Migration decisions based on aggregate scores alone miss substantial bidirectional item-level change. The complete response-level archive and per-item scoring outputs are released.

</details>


### [82] [Reshaping the SDLC for Data- and AI-Centric Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17824)
*Mamdouh Alenezi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文论证传统以源代码为中心的SDLC难以充分覆盖数据密集型与AI系统，提出将DataOps、MLOps和LLMOps纳入生命周期，并以“工件—契约—关卡—控制—治理”五层框架构建可适应配置漂移的闭环SDLC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SDLC默认系统行为主要由代码决定，但数据密集型和AI系统的行为由代码、数据及训练模型共同塑造，且会因输入分布、数据质量和运行环境变化而退化。因此，需求定义、测试、发布和维护不能仅依赖代码审查与确定性验证，需要形成面向数据和模型的工程化生命周期。

Method: 论文采用跨领域文献综合，覆盖软件工程、数据管理、机器学习系统与人本计算，并按需求、架构、开发、测试、部署、监控、治理和组织等阶段梳理生命周期变化。在理论上，将系统行为形式化为代码、数据和模型配置的函数；将需求表述为由评估指标驱动、具有概率接受域的规范；以统计验证关卡控制版本晋级。进一步提出五层自适应框架，并建立“数据工程集成—生命周期可测结果”的概念研究模型。

Result: 论文的主要产出包括：一套分阶段的生命周期转型图景；关于代码—数据—模型协同决定行为的轻量形式化；基于统计证据的验证与发布机制；以及将维护定义为应对配置漂移的闭环控制过程的五层框架。论文同时指出，业界和学界已基本确认数据工程与AI运维会重塑SDLC，但对其实际效果大小、成本收益和因果机制的量化证据仍不足。

Conclusion: 该研究将AI与数据系统的软件生命周期从“代码交付流程”扩展为“多工件、可观测、受治理的持续适应系统”。其核心价值在于统一DataOps、MLOps和LLMOps的生命周期视角；其主要局限是以概念综合和理论框架为主，缺少充分的实证量化验证。后续研究应通过多组织、多系统的纵向研究，检验数据工程集成对质量、可靠性、交付效率、漂移恢复能力和治理成本的影响。

Abstract: The traditional Software Development Lifecycle (SDLC) assumes that system behavior is determined primarily by source code, allowing correctness to be specified, implemented, and verified through code-centric practices. Data-intensive and AI-enabled systems challenge this assumption because their behavior emerges from the interaction of code, data, and learned models, while performance may degrade as real-world conditions drift from training data. This paper examines how integrating data engineering and software engineering practices, operationalized through DataOps, MLOps, and LLMOps, reshapes the SDLC for these systems. We make four contributions. First, we synthesize literature across software engineering, data management, machine learning systems, and human-centered computing into a phase-structured account of lifecycle transformation spanning requirements, architecture, development, testing, deployment, monitoring, governance, and organization. Second, we provide a lightweight formalization in which system behavior is defined over code, data, and model configurations; requirements become evaluation-led specifications with probabilistic acceptance regions; and promotion is controlled through statistically grounded validation gates. Third, we develop an adaptive five-layer lifecycle framework comprising artifact, contract, gate, control, and governance layers, positioning maintenance as a closed-loop control problem under configuration drift. Fourth, we propose a conceptual research model linking data engineering integration to measurable lifecycle outcomes and critically assess the evidence base. While the direction of transformation is increasingly established, its magnitude remains insufficiently quantified. We conclude with a research agenda for an empirically grounded, adaptive SDLC for data- and AI-centric systems.

</details>


### [83] [What Does It Mean and Why Should I Bother? Motivating Students to Write Better Commit Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17993)
*Gergő Balogh,Péter Seres,László Tóth,Attila Szatmári,Szakács Bence,Ádám Zoltán Végh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文围绕学生提交信息常无法发挥沟通作用这一教学疑虑展开，先验证问题在学生与工业项目中均反复存在，再设计教育游戏WDYM提升学习者对沟通失效的意识；该游戏促进反思，但尚未证明能立即或持续改善提交信息写作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程教学中，提交信息应帮助协作者理解代码变更的目的、范围和背景，但教师观察到学生提交信息往往表达不清。论文旨在以实证方式检验该观察，并探索适合课程时间与组织约束的轻量级教学干预。

Method: 研究采用本地情境驱动的混合方法案例研究。首先，使用既有提交信息质量分类法的部分复现，对学生项目和工业案例项目的提交信息进行比较分析，以识别沟通与质量问题。随后设计角色扮演式教育游戏WDYM，并通过游戏过程观察和参与者问卷评估其教学效果。

Result: 提交信息分析显示，学生与工业项目中都持续出现沟通不足和质量缺陷，支持了教师的初始判断。WDYM能够帮助参与者识别提交信息中的沟通断裂，并提升其相关意识与反思水平；但对于参与者是否因此立刻改进、以及能否长期维持更高质量的提交信息写作，证据有限。

Conclusion: WDYM可作为特定课程环境中处理提交信息沟通问题的实用辅助工具，其主要价值在于意识唤起和反思促进，而非已被充分验证的行为改变机制。论文谨慎地将结论限定于所研究的本地教学场景，不主张其具有普遍适用性。

Abstract: This paper reports on a locally motivated mixed-methods case study addressing a teaching-related suspicion held by software engineering instructors: that commit messages written by students frequently fail to serve their intended communicative role. To examine this suspicion empirically, we analyzed commit messages from student and industrial case-study projects using a partial replication of an established commit-message quality taxonomy. The results confirm that communication and quality issues occur recurrently in both contexts, substantiating the instructors' initial concern. Motivated by this finding, we devised What Do You Mean? (WDYM), a lightweight, role-based educational game intended to surface and address commit-message communication breakdowns within the constraints of university coursework. Analysis of gameplay observations and participant surveys shows that WDYM is effective in raising awareness and fostering reflection on commit-message communication issues, although it provides limited evidence of immediate or sustained improvement in commit-message writing practices. Taken together, the study presents WDYM as a useful, though imperfect, context-bound intervention for addressing a locally observed instructional problem, without claiming general applicability beyond the studied setting.

</details>


### [84] [The Polyglot's Dilemma: Conformance Testing a Dozen Specs in as Many Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18039)
*A. Jesse Jiryu Davis,Jeremy Mikola,Jeff Yemin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文介绍了一种面向多语言客户端库的一致性保障方法：将测试以YAML规范一次编写，再由各语言库中的解释器执行。该方法最终演化为统一测试格式，并显著减少重复测试代码与跨库行为不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MongoDB客户端库覆盖十余种编程语言、拥有数百万行原生实现代码。由于多数库并非共享核心的简单封装，不同语言实现容易在边界行为、错误处理和功能语义上产生偏差；因此需要一种可扩展、可维护且能跨语言复用的验证机制。

Method: 作者总结了十一年的规范驱动测试实践：先将预期行为表达为声明式YAML测试用例，再在每种语言的客户端库中实现对应的测试解释器；随后将原先多个临时测试格式逐步整合为Unified Test Format。论文还讨论了声明式测试设计、解释器与测试框架的架构、模式演进和统一化边界等工程问题。

Result: 统一测试格式使项目删除了超过22,000行测试代码，说明跨语言测试逻辑和格式复用有效降低了维护成本。采用YAML测试的客户端库中，行为不符合规范的缺陷发生率最高下降86%，但不同库的改善幅度存在差异。

Conclusion: 规范驱动、声明式且可解释执行的跨语言测试体系，能够有效提升多实现系统的一致性并降低测试重复度。但统一格式并非适用于所有测试场景，测试语言的表达能力、模式兼容性、语言特性差异和解释器维护成本决定了其实际适用边界。

Abstract: MongoDB maintains client libraries in a dozen programming languages, used by tens of thousands of organizations and millions of developers. Most are implemented natively rather than as wrappers around a shared core. Ensuring consistent behavior across these libraries, comprising millions of lines of code, is hard but essential. Over eleven years, we developed a specification-based testing approach: tests are written once in YAML and executed by language-specific interpreters for each library. We describe the evolution from many ad-hoc formats to a Unified Test Format, which allowed us to delete over 22,000 lines of test code. The rate of nonconformance bugs fell up to 86% in drivers that adopted YAML tests (though results varied). We report lessons learned about declarative test design, test architecture, schema evolution, and the limits of unification.

</details>


### [85] [The Working Set of a Coding Agent: Coherence Debt in Repository-Scale Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16630)
*Bardia Mohammadi,Lars Klein,Aman Chadha,Akhil Arora,Laurent Bindschaedler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文将仓库级编程建模为“耦合事实图”重建问题：编辑能否成功主要取决于所需事实在上下文或参数记忆中是否可得，而非事实与编辑位置的距离或智能体读取了多少内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体评测常以读取次数、上下文规模或最终测试通过率衡量能力，但难以区分模型是依赖当前上下文、参数记忆，还是在事实缺失时进行猜测。作者旨在量化事实可用性、记忆替代阅读及评测框架设计对仓库级任务成功率与成本的影响。

Method: 作者在7个模型和5种执行框架上，系统性控制两类事实来源：近期上下文与参数记忆；通过提供或隐去事实、对真实库进行重命名以破坏记忆、以及注入配置和迁移等故障，观察任务完成、测试覆盖、令牌消耗、读取行为与阻塞声明。实验还扩展至SWE-bench，以考察模型可能已记忆目标仓库时读取指标的有效性。

Result: 当未知API且上下文与参数记忆均不可用时，没有模型能完成任务；将所需事实直接提供给提示词可恢复成功。重命名库后，7个模型在相同位置失败且漏过相同测试，表明其依赖已记忆知识。事实缺失造成的损失与该事实支撑的工作量对应，而与其距编辑位置远近无关；远处提供的事实与邻近事实同样有效。不同框架即使均通过测试，令牌开销仍可相差十倍以上，且在事实缺失时增加开销无法挽回结果。模型通常会编造文件或猜测值，而非停止执行，因此仅以读取行为检测缺失信息会失效。标准文档与现有代码冲突时，模型倾向遵循标准，即便会产生较差代码；在SWE-bench中，参数记忆会削弱“读取行为预测成功”的关系。

Conclusion: 仓库级智能体的关键瓶颈是写入时依赖事实的可用性，而不是检索距离或表面上的阅读量。执行框架应在编辑发生时保证测试、导入、配置和迁移规则等关键事实可访问，并依据生成代码是否真正反映这些事实来审计可用性；同时应警惕陈旧规范、参数记忆泄漏和模型在信息不足时的主动臆造。

Abstract: Repository-scale coding requires an agent to keep tests, imports, configuration, and migration rules consistent within a bounded context window. We model this as reconstructing a coupled-fact graph: at each edit, a required fact comes from recent context or parametric memory, and the facts covered by neither form coherence debt. We supply and withhold each channel and inject faults across seven models and five harnesses. As expected, no model completes a task on an unseen API with both channels empty, and putting the facts in the prompt restores success. When a rename defeats what models memorized about a real library, all seven fail in the same place, passing and missing the same tests. Availability decides the outcome and distance does not: withholding a fact costs exactly the work it supports, and a supplied fact works as well far from the edit as next to it. Harnesses pay unequal prices for it: configurations that all pass every test differ more than tenfold in tokens consumed because they rebuild the same content at different rates, and spending more recovers nothing when facts are withheld. A missing fact produces wrong work rather than absent work: an agent asked to act acts, fabricating the file or guessing the value, so instruments built on reads look for a hole already filled. How often it says it is blocked instead is a property of the model, from every trial to none. Availability does not settle every edit: where standard and code disagree, agents follow the standard even when it prescribes the worse code, so a stale convention file costs more than no file. Because parametric memory substitutes for reading, on SWE-bench, where models likely know the repositories, reads no longer predict success. Harnesses should keep the facts an edit depends on available when the agent writes, and check that availability against what the agent produces rather than what it reads.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Quantum-Safe Web Service Architecture Using Time-Based One-Time Passwords](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16961)
*Abel C. H. Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向系统间互操作的量子安全Web服务架构，将基于后量子密码学的TLS/HTTPS传输保护与基于HMAC的时间型一次性密码（TOTP）认证结合，并比较多种哈希算法在不同硬件资源下的计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OTP主要用于用户登录场景，但自动化连接和系统对系统的Web服务访问同样需要可靠认证。与此同时，现有TLS/HTTPS密码机制面临未来量子计算攻击风险，因此需要构建兼顾传输安全、服务认证与部署性能的量子安全方案。

Method: 研究设计了量子安全Web服务架构：在传输层采用后量子密码学支持的TLS和HTTPS实现安全通信；在服务认证层使用HMAC构造TOTP，以验证自动化访问请求。实验部分在不同硬件资源配置下，测试并比较SHA-2、SHA-3、Ascon-Hash256和SM3的计算耗时，以评估其用于TOTP生成及Web服务部署的适用性。

Result: 摘要表明，研究完成了量子安全传输机制与HMAC-TOTP服务认证机制的集成，并对四种哈希算法在不同硬件条件下的计算时间进行了分析。该摘要未给出各算法的具体性能数据、排序结果或最优部署配置。

Conclusion: 该工作说明，采用后量子TLS/HTTPS保护通信、采用HMAC-TOTP保护Web服务访问，是构建量子安全系统间服务架构的可行路径。哈希算法选择应结合目标硬件的计算能力和部署需求进行权衡；但其实际安全性、协议兼容性、密钥生命周期管理及大规模环境下的性能仍需依据完整实验结果进一步验证。

Abstract: One-Time Passwords (OTPs) have become a common option for multi-factor authentication in several applications. For instance, during website login processes, OTPs are often used in conjunction with traditional text-based usernames and passwords to verify whether the access request originates from a legitimate human user rather than an automated agent. However, in scenarios involving automated connections and system-to-system interoperability, Time-Based One-Time Passwords (TOTPs) may be required to establish secure connections and access Web Services (WSs). Therefore, this study focuses on exploring the development of a quantum-safe web service architecture. The proposed approach achieves transmission security management by implementing Transport Layer Security (TLS) and HyperText Transfer Protocol Secure (HTTPS) based on Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC). Furthermore, web service security management is realized through the construction of keyed-Hash Message Authentication Code (HMAC)-driven TOTPs. Within the experimental environment, this study evaluates and compares the computational performance of the Secure Hash Algorithm-2 (SHA-2), SHA-3, Ascon-Hash256, and SM3. The required computation time under different hardware resource conditions is analyzed for future web service deployment.

</details>


### [87] [Probing the Prefill: Detecting Code Vulnerabilities via Latent Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16970)
*Alizishaan Khatri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究冻结的大语言模型隐藏激活是否包含C/C++代码缺陷状态信号，并通过轻量级MLP探针验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码缺陷检测通常在代码生成完成后进行，依赖静态分析、微调分类器或外部模型评审。作者希望探索模型内部表征能否提供更早、更轻量且与模型原生状态相关的检测信号。

Method: 作者从4个不同系列的大语言模型中提取代码输入阶段最后一个预填充词元的隐藏激活，并在其上训练MLP探针。实验覆盖Devign、Big-Vul、Draper VDISC和PrimeVul四个函数级C/C++数据集；探针参数量为1340万至1600万，低于基础模型规模的0.2%。

Result: 探针平均F1为41.7%。在Devign上，Qwen3.5-9B对应探针取得68.8% F1，与已发表的微调分类器最佳结果67.9%相当；但在类别更不均衡、难度更高的Big-Vul、Draper VDISC和PrimeVul上，探针结果显著落后于现有最佳方法。

Conclusion: 冻结通用代码模型的内部激活确实编码了与代码缺陷状态相关的信息，且可通过小型探针读取。这为模型原生、低开销的代码缺陷筛查提供了初步证据，但跨数据集泛化能力和不均衡场景性能仍是主要限制。

Abstract: LLM-based code generation is now embedded in mission-critical pipelines, but defenses against vulnerable output remain post-hoc -- static analyzers, fine-tuned classifiers, or an LLM judge that screen completed code, ignoring the generating model's own internal state. We test a narrower, directly measurable question: when an LLM reads a piece of C/C++ code as context, do its hidden activations already carry a signal about that code's vulnerability status? We extract last prefill token activations from four LLMs (Granite-4.1-8B, Qwen3.5-9B, Qwen3.6-27B, Gemma-4-12B) across three model families and train MLP probes on these activations. We evaluate them on four function-level C/C++ benchmarks (Devign, Big-Vul, Draper VDISC, PrimeVul). Our probes achieve 41.7\% average F1 using 13.4--16.0M-parameter probes -- under 0.2\% of base-model size. On Devign, the best probe (Qwen3.5-9B, 68.8\% F1) matches the published fine-tuned-classifier SOTA (67.9\%) despite reading only a frozen, general-purpose LLM's activations; on the harder, more imbalanced benchmarks (Big-Vul, Draper VDISC, PrimeVul) probes trail SOTA substantially. This is early evidence that a coding LLM's own representation of arbitrary code is informative about that code's vulnerability status, motivating further work toward lightweight, model-native vulnerability screening.

</details>


### [88] [Remote-Timer-as-a-Service: Efficient Microarchitectural Leakage in the Cloud with Remote Timers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17043)
*Martin Schwarzl,Haocheng Xiao,Albert Pedersen,Sam Ainsworth,Nigel Topham*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示了多租户边缘计算平台在取消进程隔离、依赖语言级隔离后，仍可能遭受跨租户推测执行侧信道风险。作者在生产环境中验证了远程数据泄露，并推动平台部署多层缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘函数平台为降低冷启动延迟而让多个租户共享进程上下文。尽管平台已限制计时器、共享内存和多线程，并通过动态进程隔离识别可疑脚本，但这些措施是否足以阻断基于推测执行的跨租户信息泄露尚不明确。

Method: 作者评估动态进程隔离机制的实际防护能力，利用微架构信号放大与远程计时能力验证生产环境中的可观测时间差异；随后构造端到端跨租户侧信道实验，在同机部署的受害函数中验证敏感令牌泄露的可行性，并比较泄露速率和准确率。

Result: 研究发现，单纯冻结或粗化计时器不足以消除风险，原有动态进程隔离实现也存在检测不足。实验表明，攻击的数据提取能力从已有工作的约2比特/分钟提升至最高12比特/秒，准确率达到99.16%，证明该问题会对租户数据隔离造成现实威胁。

Conclusion: 论文说明，低延迟多租户边缘运行时不能仅依赖计时器限制和行为检测来防御推测执行侧信道。平台随后采用V8 Sandbox限制瞬态访问范围、增强动态进程隔离检测，并引入基于MPK的进程内堆隔离。核心启示是应结合运行时沙箱、硬件内存保护与更严格的租户内存边界，形成纵深防御。

Abstract: Edge computing solutions have become a crucial part of the industry, delivering fast, flexible and scalable applications close to the end users, with typical use cases including dynamic content creation, image resizing and chatbots. Cloudflare Workers is one such framework, which handles millions of HTTP requests per second worldwide. To reduce start-up latency, Cloudflare Workers removes process-isolation boundaries between multiple tenants and leverages language-level isolation. This architecture poses the risk of Spectre attacks. To mitigate these, Cloudflare Workers previously introduced several countermeasures such as restricted timer measurements, no shared memory, no multithreading and Dynamic Process Isolation (DyPrIs), detecting potential attacks and process-isolating potentially malicious scripts.
  We demonstrate that the production implementation of DyPrIs was insufficient. We adopt microarchitectural amplification techniques and discover various possibilities to measure time in the production environment of Cloudflare Workers. Given these techniques, we show that freezing and coarsening timers in the Cloudflare Workers security model is insufficient. Leveraging both timing amplification and remote timers, we demonstrate a remote Spectre attack that leaks a JWT token from a co-located victim worker in the Cloudflare Workers production environment. We outperform the existing attack by orders of magnitude, going from 2 bit/min to up to 12 bit/s at an accuracy of 99.16%, posing an immediate risk to customer data. Following our end-to-end attack, Cloudflare Workers mitigated it in a coordinated effort by integrating the V8 Sandbox limiting transient access to 64-bit pointers, improving the detection capabilities of DyPrIs, and deploying hardware-assisted MPK-based in-process isolation to confine each tenant heap under a dedicated memory-protection key.

</details>


### [89] [CUSTOS: Toward Forensic-Ready Zero Trust at the Capture-Containment Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17068)
*Avinash Srinivasan,John Paramadilok*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向取证的零信任参考架构CUSTOS，通过取证管理点（FMP）协调持续决策留痕、分层采集、身份与策略关联重建及受零信任保护的调查访问，以缓解加密通信、短生命周期工作负载和自动化处置导致的证据缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零信任架构以持续验证替代隐式信任，但mTLS降低载荷可见性，临时工作负载削弱基于IP的归因，自动驱逐压缩易失证据采集窗口。现有安全控制虽可阻断风险，却未必能保留可验证、可重建的取证证据。

Method: 作者设计以FMP为中心的CUSTOS架构：在网关持续捕获并哈希链式保护决策记录；在事件触发后执行受控检查点与易失内存采集；以身份、策略和遥测数据重建决策上下文；并通过零信任机制限制调查访问。实验包括实时执行网关、运行时实验、编排器实验、容器SIGKILL竞争测试，以及五个公开基准数据集与合成模式的遥测覆盖率评估。

Result: 网关端始终在线的决策记录对进程内策略引擎仅产生1.9%至3.0%的吞吐开销。反应式检查点约需65毫秒，快于数秒级防御驱逐，但无法应对约9毫秒的非顺序SIGKILL、内核级强制执行和对抗性自毁。在真实容器中，并发采集与SIGKILL时，1000次试验均未恢复植入秘密；将SIGKILL置于FMP屏障之后时，1000次均恢复成功。身份导向遥测可填充决策记录模式的64%至75%，高于网络导向遥测的18%至30%；全内存采集的接纳量则受速率限制约束。

Conclusion: 取证就绪的零信任不能仅依赖事后反应式采集，而需要建立持续、低开销且可验证的证据底座，并配合有界的反应式检查点机制。FMP能够通过协调处置顺序显著提升易失证据保全率，但对于未受控的快速终止、内核级操作和自毁行为，仍存在不可恢复的证据窗口，即“取证粉碎机”效应。

Abstract: Zero Trust (ZT) replaces implicit trust with continuous verification, but mutual TLS, ephemeral workloads, identity-centric control, and automated remediation reduce payload visibility, weaken IP-based attribution, and shrink the window for acquiring volatile evidence. We propose CUSTOS, a forensic-ready ZT reference architecture centered on a Forensic Management Point (FMP) that coordinates tiered capture, identity- and policy-linked reconstruction, telemetry orchestration, and ZT-controlled investigative access. We evaluate a composed, component-level prototype using a live enforcement gateway plus separate runtime and orchestrator experiments. An always-on decision record is captured and hash-chained on the gateway at a 1.9-3.0\% throughput cost on in-process policy engines, preserving decision provenance outside the monitored workload under stated trust assumptions. Reactive checkpointing (about 65 ms) precedes seconds-scale defender-routed eviction but loses to unsequenced direct SIGKILL (about 9 ms), in-kernel enforcement, and adversarial self-destruction, producing the forensic shredder effect. On a real container, concurrent capture and SIGKILL recovered the planted secret in 0/1000 trials; sequencing SIGKILL behind the FMP barrier recovered it in 1000/1000. The primary integrated single-node Kubernetes race checkpoints an FMP-controlled process; container-memory capture is evaluated separately and was unavailable in the managed-Kubernetes configuration. Across five public benchmark datasets and a synthetic schema reference, identity-oriented telemetry populates 64-75\% of the decision-record schema against 18-30\% for network-oriented, while rate limiting bounds the full-memory admission ceiling. These results show that forensic-ready ZT requires both an always-on evidentiary floor and bounded reactive capture, while identifying where volatile evidence remains unrecoverable.

</details>


### [90] [SentryBus: A Multi-Vantage Observability Model and Validated Instrument for I2C Sensor-Interface Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17082)
*Sandesh More,Elton Batista,Karla Daley,Sneha Sudhakaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SentryBus，用于在主机侧被动监测I2C传感器总线采集行为，以识别传感器与处理器之间接口操纵可能留下的异常证据，并界定不同攻击位置下可观测性的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式系统通常默认到达处理器的传感器测量值可信，但位于传感器数字接口上的攻击者可注入表面合理的数据，使正常固件将其作为常规遥测上报。因此，需要研究采集路径是否保留可被被动监测器利用的痕迹，以及何时攻击会与合法采集不可区分。

Method: SentryBus对I2C采集行为建模，特征包括事务时序、读写序列、传输长度、地址行为、寄存器与FIFO状态访问及原始数据转变。论文考虑内联中继、并行控制器、传感器替换和主机受损等攻击位置，并指出普通仅作为目标设备的传感器无法主动发起、延展、重排或延迟总线事务。实验采用双侧采集平台记录两侧总线、主机内存和遥测；检测器仅使用主机侧总线，其他观测点用于提供真实状态对照。

Result: 生理传感器实例显示：内联中继在保持采集调度与载荷内容时，其可隐藏空间被界定为采集服务时间的0.842%；遥测观测点上的正常采集连续稳定运行6304秒，未发生时钟回归；原始数据转变特征包含会话特定的信号统计特性，无法跨采集会话直接迁移。此外，仪器评估发现低成本分析仪可能截断采集记录而不产生内核可见错误。由于受控攻击试验尚未完成，论文未报告检测率。

Conclusion: 该工作主要贡献在于提出以主机侧I2C行为为基础的被动检测框架、明确攻击能力与观测位置的关系，并通过初步测量给出内联操纵的时间边界及数据特征跨会话失效这一负面结果。其证据目前更支持可观测性和测量可行性，而非实际检测效能；后续需开展受控攻击实验、量化误报与漏报，并采用会话稳健或自适应的特征设计。

Abstract: Sensor-driven systems in medical Internet of Things devices, drones, and cyber-physical systems commonly trust a measurement once it reaches the embedded processor. An adversary on the digital interface between sensor and processor can supply a plausible value that correct firmware accepts and reports as ordinary telemetry. The hypothesis is that sensor interface manipulation leaves observable evidence on the acquisition path, that the evidence appears at different vantages depending on attacker position, and that a passive host-side monitor therefore has a measurable boundary beyond which manipulation becomes indistinguishable from legitimate acquisition. SentryBus models acquisition behavior on the I2C sensor bus using transaction timing, read and write sequences, transfer lengths, address behavior, register and FIFO state access, and raw data transitions. The adversary is modeled as an inline interposer, parallel controller, sensor replacement, or compromised host, because a commodity target-only sensor cannot initiate transfers or stretch, reorder, or delay bus transactions. A dual sided testbed captures both busses, host memory, and telemetry, and the detector consumes the host facing bus alone while the remaining vantages serve as ground truth. A physiological instantiation reports three measured results: an inline interposer bounded at 0.842 percent of acquisition service time while preserving acquisition schedule and payload content, clean acquisition stability sustained over 6304 seconds at the telemetry vantage with no clock regression, and a negative result establishing that data-transition features encode session specific signal statistics and do not transfer across capture sessions. Instrument characterization shows that a low-cost analyzer can truncate captures without kernel visible error. Controlled attack trials are still outstanding, so no detection rate is claimed.

</details>


### [91] [Structured Driving-State Narratives for Small Language Model-Based GNSS Spoofing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17092)
*Abyad Enan,Sagar Dasgupta,Mizanur Rahman,Mashrur Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于小语言模型（SLM）的GNSS欺骗攻击检测与分类框架，通过比较GNSS与其他传感源独立推导的车辆状态，实现面向自动驾驶车辆的实时、低资源安全监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆高度依赖GNSS定位，而欺骗信号能够生成表面合理但实际错误的位置与运动状态。现有高性能语言模型通常计算和显存开销较大，不利于部署在车载等资源受限平台，因此需要验证更轻量的SLM是否能在保持检测精度的同时降低部署成本。

Method: 框架首先分别从GNSS和其他传感器数据中推导车辆行驶状态，再将这些状态转换为结构化语义叙述并输入SLM，以识别是否存在欺骗及其攻击类型。实验将SLM与使用相同训练数据微调的LLM进行对比，并在统一测试集上评估五类状态：无攻击、过冲攻击、停车攻击、逐路段转向攻击和错误转向攻击。此外，研究还采用来自未见地理区域的实测数据验证泛化能力。

Result: SLM总体性能接近LLM，平均准确率为96.99%，精确率为99.05%，召回率为95.59%，F1值为97.18%。相较于LLM，SLM在微调和推理阶段均具有更低的推理延迟和更少的GPU显存需求。使用来自不同地理区域的实测数据进行测试时，框架仍表现出有效的检测与分类能力。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，基于结构化车辆状态语义叙述的SLM框架能够高精度地检测和分类GNSS欺骗攻击，并具备较好的跨区域泛化性。由于其在计算延迟和内存资源上的优势，该方法适合部署于资源受限的车载计算平台，用于实时定位安全监测。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) depend on reliable Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) positioning. However, spoofed GNSS signals can induce plausible but incorrect vehicle states. This study develops a small language model (SLM)-based framework for detecting and classifying GNSS spoofing attacks by comparing vehicle behaviors independently derived from GNSS and other sensing sources. The framework converts independent driving states from GNSS and other sensing sources into structured semantic narratives that are provided to an SLM for spoofing detection and attack classification. The performance of the SLM-based framework is compared with large language models (LLMs) fine-tuned on identical training data and evaluated on the same test set. The evaluation considers five classes: no attack, overshoot attack, stopped attack, turn-by-turn attack, and wrong-turn attack. The framework is also evaluated with geographically unseen field data collected in Clemson, South Carolina, United States. Experimental results indicate that the evaluated SLMs achieve performance similar to the LLMs, achieving an average accuracy of 96.99%, precision of 99.05%, recall of 95.59%, and F1-score of 97.18%. In terms of computational efficiency and resource utilization, the SLMs demonstrate advantages over the LLMs by requiring lower inference latency and less GPU memory during both fine-tuning and inference. Evaluation using field data collected in a geographically distinct location further demonstrated its efficacy. The presented framework can detect and classify GNSS spoofing attacks in real-time while requiring relatively low computational and memory resources, and is therefore suitable for deployment on resource-constrained vehicular computing platforms.

</details>


### [92] [Digital Twin-Based Intrusion Detection for Vehicle Powertrain CAN Bus Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17093)
*Araf Rahman,M Sabbir Salek,Mashrur Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于数字孪生的车载CAN入侵检测系统，通过学习动力总成信号之间的耦合关系，检测通信时序正常但载荷被篡改的隐蔽异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAN入侵检测方法主要依赖报文时间、频率和序列特征，因此难以发现保持正常通信模式、仅修改数据载荷的异常。传统数字孪生多用于仿真和测试，尚未充分用于实时检测。

Method: 研究使用真实车辆CAN日志中的17个已解码信号训练共享编码器LSTM数字孪生模型，在24步时间窗口内联合预测7个数值信号和2个档位类别信号。系统根据预测值与观测值之间的残差及校准阈值进行告警，并采用自适应滚动策略避免持续异常污染模型输入历史。实验将该方法与基于范围和合理性规则的基线方法进行比较，覆盖平台型、连续漂移型、伪装型和档位伪装型四类载荷篡改场景。

Result: 数字孪生方法在全部测试场景中优于基线。对于连续漂移和伪装场景，检测率分别达到94.6%和89.2%；基线方法对多数伪造载荷几乎无法识别。结果表明，联合建模车辆物理动态和信号关联能够有效识别不改变CAN通信节奏的载荷异常。但在持续异常条件下，误报率最高达到39.6%，说明系统鲁棒性仍有明显改进空间。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了数字孪生用于CAN载荷级异常检测的可行性，尤其适合发现规避时序、频率和序列检测的隐蔽篡改。后续工作应重点降低持续异常下的误报率，并增强模型对工况变化、传感器噪声和长期数据分布漂移的适应能力。

Abstract: Existing automotive intrusion detection systems (IDSs) for the Controller Area Network (CAN) largely target discrepancies in message timing, frequency, or sequencing and cannot detect attacks that preserve these properties while manipulating the payload. Digital twins (DTs) have been used to emulate CAN traffic and generate attack scenarios for IDS evaluation, but their use for intrusion detection remains unexplored. This study develops a DT-based IDS that jointly models physical relationships among decoded powertrain signals and identifies attacks through residuals between predicted and observed behavior. A shared-encoder LSTM DT was trained on 17 decoded signals from a real Hyundai/Kia CAN log to jointly predict seven numeric and two categorical gear signals over a 24-step window. A timestep is flagged when a residual exceeds a calibrated threshold, while adaptive rollout protects the twin's input history from sustained contamination. Four attacks (plateau, continuous drift, masquerade, and gear masquerade) were evaluated against the twin and a range-and-plausibility baseline. The DT outperformed the baseline across all attacks, achieving detection rates of 94.6% for continuous drift and 89.2% for masquerade, while the baseline detected almost none of the fabricated payload attacks. These results demonstrate that learning coupled vehicle dynamics enables detection of stealthy payload manipulations that preserve normal CAN communication patterns. False positive rates reached 39.6%, highlighting the need for improved robustness under sustained attacks. The DT-based IDS shows promise for detecting stealthy payload-level CAN attacks that preserve normal communication patterns, supporting behavior-based cybersecurity for connected and automated vehicles.

</details>


### [93] [Protocol-Embedded Compliance for Privacy-Preserving, Non-Custodial Digital Payments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17145)
*Santiago De Simone,Geoffrey Goodell,Georgios Samakovitis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种非托管、类似现金的数字资产支付架构，旨在无需依赖账户托管或中心化身份控制的前提下，实现交易合规、可审计性与隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统支付基础设施通常认为，交易完整性、审计、身份核验与合规治理必须依赖托管式账户体系及指定中介机构。作者质疑这一前提，试图证明数字资产可通过协议规则和密码学证明，在保留用户隐私的同时承担合规验证功能。

Method: 论文以USO（Unforgeable, Stateful, Oblivious）资产模型为基础，设计参考架构与核心协议规则。其关键机制是将合规信息作为由独立实体签发的密码学签名证明，直接嵌入数字资产状态；资产的创建、转移和赎回必须满足适用的合规谓词，并将验证结果的签名写入资产状态。系统中的参与者、角色和组件被明确划分，以支持对交易合规性的独立验证。

Result: 该方案将合规执行从机构托管和基于身份的账户控制，转移至资产协议层。交易是否有效取决于资产状态中是否包含满足规则的有效证明，因此可在非托管模式下提供可验证的合规性与审计能力，同时避免持续暴露完整用户身份信息。作者进一步认为，该模型能够与现有支付系统对接。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：合规数字货币交换并不必然要求托管账户和中心化控制。通过状态化数字资产、独立签发的密码学证明及协议级有效性规则，非托管数字资产可实现隐私保护与合规可验证性的结合，并具备接入传统金融基础设施的潜力。

Abstract: Received wisdom on payments infrastructure strongly supports the custodial, account-based model as a necessity for transaction integrity, auditability and verification; the set of fundamental primitives for regulated digital money exchange, the argument goes, necessitates designated identifiable entities that store and process credentials, perform KYC, and ultimately act as the 'single version of the truth' for compliance remediation and, most important, AML. In this paper, we propose this is not the case, by arguing that non-custodial, cash-like digital assets can embody such capabilities, in an arguably more secure manner.
  To that end, we present a reference architecture and core protocol rules for digital-value-exchange systems that preserve meaningful user privacy while enabling strong auditability. The protocol defines the conditions under which digital asset creation, transfer, and redemption are valid. The architecture specifies the allocation of actors, roles and components through which these rules operate, enabling independent verification of transaction compliance with applicable norms. Building upon the Unforgeable, Stateful, Oblivious (USO) asset model of Goodell et al., regulatory compliance data are embedded directly into the asset state as cryptographically signed attestations issued by independent entities. A transfer is valid only upon satisfaction of applicable compliance predicates and inclusion of the resulting signature within the asset state. Compliance enforcement is thus performed at the protocol level rather than through institutional custody or identity-based account control. We conclude that our proposed model can successfully interface with existing payment systems, making it possible to integrate non-custodial, compliance-verified transactions with legacy financial infrastructure.

</details>


### [94] [Picture the Epsilon: Pursuing Identity-Level Privacy Guarantees for Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17147)
*Arman Zareian Jahromi,Vishnu Bondalakunta,Mohammad Akbar Bin Shah,Naimul Haque,Shuangqing Wei,George T. Amariucai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Image-to-image face generators are widely used, and visual dissimilarity between their outputs and source images is sometimes treated as evidence of privacy. Auditing whether these systems satisfy formal identity-level (epsilon, delta)-differential privacy requires choosing among several distinct routes for converting embedding-space observations into estimates or bounds on the differential privacy parameter epsilon. We present a comparative study of four such audits applicable to pre-trained, black-box face generators: a Gaussian-mechanism reading of per-identity sensitivity (GaussMech); a per-dimension kernel-density log-ratio aggregated by basic composition (KDE-LR); an analytical population-level lower bound on pure-DP epsilon derived from the maximum mean discrepancy via the total variation distance (MMD-TV); and a hypothesis-testing evaluation of a cross-validated classifier's out-of-fold ROC (ROC-HT). For each method we make explicit its assumptions, hyperparameter dependence, finite-sample limitations, and the regime in which its epsilon estimate is informative. Applied to FaceFusion and InstantID across multiple identity encoders and reference datasets, the audits consistently reveal substantial identity distinguishability while reporting markedly different epsilon estimates that reflect each method's distinct assumptions and finite-sample treatment. In this high-distinguishability regime, the experiments do not support a reliable ranking of the four methods. Their relative trade-offs should be evaluated on partially private mechanisms, which we identify as the natural next study. The resulting framework places these audits in a shared identity-level audit setting and clarifies how their assumptions and finite-sample treatments shape the resulting differential privacy estimates.

</details>


### [95] [Authorization Before Context: A Model-Neutral Audience Boundary Against Cross-Audience Memory Leakage in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17148)
*Sibo Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: A personal language agent learns a fact from one audience and may later place it in the prompt it assembles for another. This memory-to-context step is an attack surface: ambiguous or inconsistent channels, cross-audience prying, and poisoned memory can each cause the system to assemble context containing a fact relevant to the query yet unauthorized for the current viewers. We introduce authorization before context: a single, anti-monotone audience-membership rule applied at the memory-to-context transition. Each item carries the audience present when it was recorded; the current viewer set is read from channel metadata and falls back to public when ambiguous; and the item is admitted only when every current viewer already belonged to its audience. We prove that this rule gives every participant cross-channel recall while ensuring, by exclusion rather than by model behavior, that nothing recorded for a narrower audience reaches a broader one and that poisoned memory cannot widen its own audience. The boundary is a model-neutral invariant on the exact assembled context: a forbidden fact must be absent before the model is called. On a synthetic Contextual-Integrity suite, no forbidden fact entered the context our boundary assembled, whereas unscoped baselines included such facts by construction; we further audit that every read path fails closed. The evidence is preliminary and synthetic.

</details>


### [96] [Beyond the Hype: Evaluating LLM Integration and Practical Limitations in Security Operation Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17154)
*Elnaz Rabieinejad,Ali Dehghantanha,Fattane Zarrinkalam,Sarina Dastgerdy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored within Security Operation Centers (SOCs) to support text-heavy analytical work such as alert contextualization, incident summarization, and drafting investigative artifacts. Despite this interest, practitioners describe critical operational concerns, most notably hallucinations (plausible but incorrect outputs), opaque reasoning, and the verification effort required to safely use model-generated content in security workflows. In this paper, we present findings from semi-structured interviews with 20 SOC practitioners spanning frontline analysts, SOC managers, and tool developers. Participants report perceived time savings for low-stakes tasks that are quickly verifiable (e.g., summarizing logs or drafting initial investigative leads), but they consistently frame LLM outputs as preliminary drafts and suggestions rather than decision-grade conclusions. Participants also describe limited trust in LLMs for high-stakes security decisions due to unreliable outputs and unclear model reasoning, and they report relying primarily on ad-hoc verification norms and continuous human oversight rather than standardized mitigation procedures. Based on these interview-grounded accounts, we introduce a maturity rubric to characterize readiness for LLM integration and outline a research agenda emphasizing auditability and transparent explanation mechanisms to support safer adoption in SOC workflows.

</details>


### [97] [The Acknowledgment Point Is the System: Durable Policy-Decision Receipts for AI Audit Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17176)
*Neeraj Kumar Singh Beshane*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究重构了 RuntimeGuard-AI，明确审计记录的持久性与信任边界，并通过签名回执、崩溃恢复校验及 Merkle 纪元实现可验证审计。实验表明，审计强度与系统吞吐量、延迟之间存在显著权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅在持久化写入前返回受保护决策可以降低延迟，但无法保证系统立即崩溃后证据仍然存在。因此，需要明确同步边界、证据持久性和审计信任假设，避免将异步审计误认为无成本方案。

Method: 系统将每个确定性策略决策绑定至精确策略源，生成隐私最小化记录，并根据调用方选择的同步边界提交记录。随后返回 Ed25519 签名回执，明确该边界是否完成。重启后，系统验证分帧记录、清单、分片位置、序列连续性和重放身份；独立证明路径则将已提交记录组织为链式签名 Merkle 纪元，并由持有外部密钥的审计方验证。

Result: 在 Apple M4 Pro、4 个工作线程和 2,048 字节提示下，缓冲式签名证据达到 27,193 请求/秒，中位延迟为 141.9 微秒。逐记录持久化和完整同步将吞吐量降至约 242 请求/秒，并使中位延迟升至 16.0 毫秒。对包含 100,000 条记录的签名纪元进行封存耗时 97.0 毫秒。

Conclusion: 该原型量化展示了审计持久性与性能之间的权衡：更强的持久化保证会显著降低吞吐量并增加延迟。系统能够验证记录完整性、连续性和外部可审计性，但不能证明模型确实执行、阻止受损签名方分叉历史，或证明符合法律要求。

Abstract: An AI audit record is useful only if its durability and trust boundary are explicit. Returning a guarded decision before any durable write minimizes latency, but it cannot guarantee that evidence survives an immediate crash. We rebuild RuntimeGuard-AI around this constraint. The resulting research prototype binds each deterministic policy decision to the exact policy source, commits a privacy-minimizing record at a caller-selected synchronization boundary, and returns an Ed25519-signed receipt that states whether that boundary completed. After restart, the engine validates framed records, manifests, shard placement, sequence continuity, and replay identity. A separate attestation path groups committed records into chained, signed Merkle epochs that an auditor verifies with an externally obtained key. On an Apple M4 Pro at four worker threads and 2,048-byte prompts, buffered signed evidence reaches 27,193 requests/s with 141.9 microseconds median latency. Per-record data and full synchronization reduce throughput to approximately 242 requests/s and raise median latency to 16.0 ms. Sealing a 100,000-record signed epoch takes 97.0 ms. The result is a measured durability-latency trade-off, not a "free" asynchronous audit path. The prototype does not prove model execution, prevent a compromised signer from forking history, or establish legal conformity.

</details>


### [98] [PACE: Policy-Attested Contract Execution for Safe AI Agents in Decentralized Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17220)
*Rabimba Karanjai,Yang Lu,Richard Williamson,Hemanth Hm,Prakhar Mehrotra,Lei Xu,Weidong,Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PACE 是面向基于大语言模型的链上交易代理的交易级授权框架，通过类型化意图、确定性策略验证和签名决策记录，将审核结果密码学绑定到实际提交的交易字节，以降低提示注入和交易篡改风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理可规划交换、借贷和收益管理等操作，但容易受到提示注入影响，且传统审核机制无法保证已批准的交易与最终链上执行的交易完全一致。

Method: 框架在代理与链上执行之间加入授权层：定义类型化交易意图；采用确定性策略验证器；生成签名的策略决策记录，将意图、策略、模拟报告与执行字节绑定，并提供重放防护和过期控制。链上通过 Solidity 智能账户验证决策记录签名。

Result: 智能账户的额外开销为29,826至31,822 gas。在包含40项任务、2,800次试验和10个随机种子的确定性沙箱中，PACE 的不安全执行率和良性任务误报率均为0.00，而无防护基线的不安全执行率为0.80。消融结果显示，宽松策略设置和接触合约白名单是主要安全因素。

Conclusion: PACE 在可复现实验基准中展示了较强的逻辑级安全性：其核心价值在于将策略审批与精确交易载荷进行可验证绑定。但作者明确指出，该结果不等同于可直接部署的安全保证；真实模型输出与主网分叉测试属于补充验证，不能替代确定性基准。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents are emerging as interfaces for decentralized finance (DeFi) actions such as swaps, lending operations, and yield management. Because these agents rely on large language models (LLMs) to plan transactions, they inherit the LLM's susceptibility to prompt injection and lack of mechanisms to bind a verifier's approval to the exact transaction ultimately submitted on-chain. We present PACE (Policy-Attested Contract Execution), a transaction-level authorization framework that interposes between an LLM-based agent and on-chain execution. PACE introduces typed transaction intents, a deterministic policy verifier, and signed Policy Decision Records (PDRs) that cryptographically bind the approved intent, policy, and simulation report to the exact execution bytes, with replay and expiration protection. A Solidity smart account enforces PDR signatures on-chain with a measured overhead of 29,826-31,822 gas. We evaluate PACE against six baselines on 40 tasks spanning four attack categories plus benign utility (2,800 trials, 10 seeds). In our deterministic sandbox, PACE achieves a 0.00 unsafe execution rate and 0.00 false-positive rate on benign tasks, compared to 0.80 for the unguarded baseline. Ablation studies identify permissive policy settings (+57.5 pp) and the touched-contract allowlist (+12.5 pp) as the dominant safety components. To test whether the same deterministic floor holds for real model outputs, the artifact additionally provides a three-model live-LLM evaluation over the full task suite with repeated runs. A mainnet-fork harness is included for archive-RPC deployments, but fork results are reported only when the corresponding artifacts are generated. These auxiliary studies are separate from, and never substitute for, the deterministic benchmark. We frame our claims as logic-level safety within a reproducible benchmark rather than deployment-ready DeFi security.

</details>


### [99] [COMIC: Reference-Aware Safety Gating for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17234)
*Md Abdullahil Oaphy,Anhao Xiang,Zongxing Xie,Huayue Gu,Chenyu Wang,Honghui Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出COMIC，一种面向多模态大语言模型的引用感知安全门控机制，通过显式建模“操作—视觉目标”对，缓解仅在视觉指代解析后才显现风险的多模态越狱问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态防御通常对文本提示和图像整体进行审核，但许多风险并不来自任一单独模态，而是由“总结、翻译、执行”等表面正常的操作与图像中局部目标绑定后产生。因此，整体审核难以识别依赖视觉指代和局部语义激活的风险。

Method: COMIC在生成前推断用户请求的操作及引用类型，结合OCR和开放词汇目标提议构建候选视觉目标，对可能的指代对象进行定位，并针对显式的操作—目标组合执行安全评估。对于指代歧义，系统采用最大风险聚合，并结合定位质量进行路由决策，以决定放行或拦截请求。

Result: 在多个开源多模态模型、局部化及更广泛的多模态越狱基准，以及良性引用敏感任务上的实验表明，COMIC能够稳定提升鲁棒性，同时较好地保持正常任务效用和实际运行效率。

Conclusion: 多模态安全防护不能仅依赖提示与图像的整体审核；可靠防御需要联合建模请求操作、其作用的视觉对象，以及视觉指代定位的置信度。COMIC为生成前的细粒度、引用感知安全控制提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interact with screenshots, scanned documents, diagrams, and other visually grounded inputs. This shift introduces a new safety risk: in many multimodal jailbreaks, neither the prompt nor the image is harmful in isolation. Unsafe behavior emerges only when the model binds an apparently benign operation, such as summarizing, translating, or following, to a localized visual target. This reveals a structural weakness in current multimodal defenses, which largely moderate the prompt-image pair as a whole even though the true security-relevant unit is the grounded operation-target pair produced during dereference. In this work, we identify and analyze this reference-dependent failure mode and show that existing defenses degrade when harmful semantics are localized, activated only after grounding, and dependent on visual reference resolution. To address this problem, we propose COMIC (Context-Operation-Modality-Image-Classifier), a reference-aware pre-generation safety gate for MLLMs. COMIC first infers the requested operation and reference type, constructs candidate targets from OCR and open-vocabulary proposals, grounds plausible referents, and evaluates safety over explicit operation-target pairs. To handle ambiguity conservatively, COMIC combines max-risk aggregation with quality-aware routing before deciding whether to forward or block a request. We evaluate COMIC across multiple open-source MLLMs, localized and broader multimodal jailbreak benchmarks, and benign reference-sensitive settings. The results show that COMIC consistently improves robustness while preserving benign utility and practical efficiency. More broadly, our findings suggest that multimodal safety cannot be enforced reliably without modeling the requested operation, the visual target to which it applies, and the confidence of that grounding.

</details>


### [100] [ADAPTD: Adaptive Detection and Proactive Threat Defense for Autonomous APT attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17251)
*Yeongwoo Kim,Quanyan Zhu,György Dán*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPTD，一个面向分段企业网络横向传播风险的去中心化防御框架。该方法联合紧凑攻击链识别、即时阻断与预测性清除，在降低通信与计算开销的同时提升检测和处置效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨子网协同防御需要全局态势感知，但频繁共享信息会带来显著通信负担；同时，静态监测与单一处置策略难以兼顾及时遏制、业务扰动控制和长期安全恢复。因此，需要具备自适应监测与灵活处置能力的决策框架。

Method: ADAPTD采用决策理论建模，通过紧凑攻击链表征多类传播路径；利用去中心化信念更新机制在有限通信条件下融合局部观测；在处置层面结合即时阻断和预测性清除两类动作，并根据环境噪声、风险状态及处置成本进行自适应选择。

Result: 实验表明：去中心化信念更新优于扩散HMM基线；相较基于Transformer的检测方案，ADAPTD显著减少错误清除；在噪声环境中，自适应阻断能够有效遏制传播并降低不必要的服务干扰；消融实验进一步验证，联合使用阻断与清除可显著降低防御方总成本。

Conclusion: ADAPTD通过将轻量级分布式态势估计与双重自适应处置机制结合，实现了检测精度、通信计算效率和处置成本之间的较优平衡。其核心贡献在于证明了即时遏制与预测性恢复的协同设计优于单一防御动作，适用于资源受限且网络分段明显的企业安全场景。

Abstract: Advanced persistent threat (APT) actors increasingly employ sophisticated techniques to propagate laterally through segmented enterprise networks. Timely detection and defense depend on cross-subnetwork coordination, yet maintaining global situational awareness generates substantial communication overhead. To manage this tradeoff, flexible monitoring and adaptable containment are imperative. This paper presents ADAPTD, a communication- and computation-efficient, decision-theoretic framework integrating: (i) compact kill chains for identifying diverse attack vectors, (ii) an immediate blocking mechanism for timely containment, and (iii) a predictive eviction strategy to restore system security. Our experiments validate ADAPTD's effectiveness across diverse threat scenarios. First, our decentralized belief update scheme outperforms state-of-the-art diffusion HMM. Second, ADAPTD substantially reduces false evictions compared to transformer-based detection. Third, under noisy environments, adaptive blocking contains attackers while minimizing unnecessary disruption. Lastly, the ablation study confirms that combining two defensive actions significantly reduces the defender's total cost.

</details>


### [101] [When Agents Act on Web3: An Attack-Surface Survey of MCP, Skills, and Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17275)
*Rabimba Karanjai,Yang Lu,Nour Diallo,Wujie Xiong,Lei Xu,Weidong,Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述具备外部状态修改能力的AI代理在MCP、技能与工具调用框架下接入公有区块链时的安全风险，指出区块链执行层会将一般代理系统中可恢复的错误放大为持续且不可逆的损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着MCP生态中可修改外部状态的工具使用比例由27%升至65%，代理获得链上执行权限的场景快速扩大。既有MCP安全研究较为分散，且传统软件安全假设难以覆盖链上交易不可逆、签名授权强约束、代理持续自主运行及交易序列可组合等特性，因此需要系统化风险框架。

Method: 论文将现有MCP安全文献按攻击面进行分类，并构建面向Web3的风险映射矩阵：针对每类攻击，关联其在链上环境中的放大后果、对应的放大因素、代表性缓解措施及剩余缺口；同时综合比较通用安全防御与新兴链上机制，并据矩阵中的未覆盖项提出研究议程。

Result: 综述认为，区块链执行层的四项核心属性显著改变了威胁模型，使提示注入、工具滥用、权限扩张或多步骤攻击等问题可能直接转化为不可逆资产或状态损失。现有防护虽有改进，但实测机制拦截的攻击比例不足30%，模型层安全拒绝率不足3%，显示单靠模型对齐或常规工具安全控制难以满足链上高后果操作的需求。

Conclusion: 该工作以攻击面分类和Web3风险映射矩阵整合MCP代理安全与链上执行风险，核心结论是：链上代理安全必须围绕最小签名权限、可验证执行、交易前约束、序列级监控及损失隔离等执行层机制设计，而非仅依赖模型拒答。文中提出的开放缺口可作为后续研究和防御评估的优先方向。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act rather than merely read: across the Model Context Protocol (MCP) ecosystem, the share of deployed tools that modify external state has risen from 27% to 65% of tool use. When agents exercise this authority on public blockchains through MCP, skills, and tool calling, the consequences of an attack are governed by the blockchain execution layer rather than by conventional software assumptions. This survey argues that four properties of that layer (irreversibility, signing authority, continuous autonomy, and sequence-level composition) qualitatively change the threat model, turning the recoverable failures of generic agent security into a standing, irreversible loss. We organize the fragmented MCP-security literature into an attack-surface taxonomy, then contribute a Web3 risk-mapping matrix that ties each attack class to its amplified impact, the responsible amplifiers, a representative mitigation, and the residual gap. We synthesize defenses, including emerging blockchain-based mechanisms, and find them improving but insufficient: measured protections stop fewer than 30% of attacks, and model-level safety refuses fewer than 3%. We close by positioning the work against adjacent surveys and deriving a research agenda from the matrix's open cells.

</details>


### [102] [FlowShield: cryptocurrency anti-money laundering with transaction semantics parsing and fund flow tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17355)
*Qishuang Fu,Andreas Deppeler,Joseph K. Liu,Yixin Liu,Shirui Pan,Qin Wang,Weiqing Wang,Tsz Hon Yuen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出FlowShield，用于多链加密资产交易的可疑洗钱检测与面向调查人员的可读报告生成；其融合行为语义、资金流子图结构和文本信息，并在新公开的多链数据集BybitML及两个公开数据集上取得平均98.0%的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常过度依赖交易拓扑，简化了交易行为语义，且多只提供孤立的检测标签，难以解释复杂、多跳、跨链的资金拆分与重分配过程。与此同时，多链场景的标注数据稀缺，也限制了模型训练和公平评估。

Method: FlowShield先从可观测交易关系中恢复行为级语义，将潜在意图显式化；随后从三个互补视角重建资金流子图，以追踪资产来源与再分配；再通过文本—结构融合机制，结合大语言模型编码的语义与资金流文本，以及图卷积网络编码的图结构，完成交易级判别。系统进一步生成可读的可疑活动报告，输出资金流摘要与可解释风险信号。论文同时构建并开源BybitML多链数据集。

Result: 在BybitML和两个公开数据集上的实验表明，FlowShield获得最佳总体性能，平均F1为98.0%。行为分析显示，该方法能够识别多样化的资产拆分、转移和多跳流转模式；报告分析表明，生成结果能够以较简洁的形式呈现复杂资金路径及其风险依据。

Conclusion: FlowShield的核心贡献在于将语义理解、图结构建模、跨链资金流追踪和报告生成整合为端到端框架。该工作不仅提升了检测准确性，也改善了结果的可解释性与调查可用性；不过，摘要未说明跨链标签构建标准、真实部署中的泛化能力、报告事实一致性及对新型规避策略的鲁棒性，仍需在完整论文中进一步验证。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency anti-money laundering (Crypto AML) is increasingly challenged by sophisticated laundering behaviors that rapidly fragment stolen assets through diverse semantics and across multiple blockchains. Existing Crypto AML methods often simplify transaction semantics, rely on topology-centric signals, or output isolated detection labels. In this paper, we present \textsc{FlowShield}, a Crypto AML framework for transaction-level laundering detection and investigator-facing report generation. \textsc{FlowShield} first recovers behavior-level semantics from observable relations, making laundering intents explicit. To trace value provenance and redistribution, \textsc{FlowShield} reconstructs fund-flow subgraphs from three complementary perspectives. It then employs a text--structure fusion mechanism, enabling the interplay between large language model (LLM)-encoded semantics and flow texts with graph convolutional network (GCN)-encoded structure. Beyond mere detection, \textsc{FlowShield} further generates readable suspicious activity reports (SARs), offering investigators concise summaries and explainable red flags. To address the data scarcity in multi-chain detection, we construct and open-source \textit{BybitML}, the first public multi-chain laundering dataset. We evaluate \textsc{FlowShield} on \textit{BybitML} and two public laundering datasets and experimental results demonstrate that \textsc{FlowShield} achieves the best overall performance, with an average F1 score of 98.0\%. Further behavior and SAR analyses demonstrate that \textsc{FlowShield} can reveal diverse laundering strategies and produce readable reports for investigating complex multi-hop fund flows.

</details>


### [103] [Fair ASR: Re-Evaluating Black-Box Jailbreaks under Shared Target-Call Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17360)
*Zhida He,Xiaoyu Wen,Han Qi,Ziyuan Zhou,Peng Yu,Jiajia Li,Chaochao Lu,Qiaosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Fair-ASR黑盒越狱评测协议，以可直接观测的目标模型调用次数作为统一预算轴，发现现有攻击在不同预算下的排名会显著变化；据此设计ReCode，在20次目标调用预算下于GPT-5达到85%攻击成功率，且平均仅需7.19次攻击者调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有越狱评测通常只报告攻击成功率，忽略成功率对攻击预算的强依赖，导致方法间比较不公平。以FLOPs统一计算资源的方案不适用于黑盒模型：FLOPs难以准确估计，也无法表达目标调用与攻击者侧调用等不同资源的实际约束。因此需要一种可观测、模型无关且可复现的公平比较基准。

Method: Fair-ASR要求各攻击方法共享目标模型调用预算B，并在该预算下比较攻击成功率；同时单独记录攻击者调用次数，以评估总体效率。作者用该协议复评11种代表性攻击，考察不同目标调用预算下的性能与排序。基于复评中识别出的低成本有效组件，提出ReCode：将去敏化改写与两种高效低成本原语进行组合，以提升有限目标调用和攻击者调用条件下的攻击效率。

Result: 实验显示，攻击方法的相对排名随目标调用预算显著改变，说明单点ASR或未统一预算的结论并不稳健。在相同目标访问条件下，简单随机扰动和人工构造模板仍具有较强竞争力；所评估的LLM驱动方法均未能同时实现目标调用和攻击者调用两方面的高效率。ReCode在20次目标调用预算下，对GPT-5取得85%的ASR，平均攻击者调用次数为7.19。

Conclusion: 公平的黑盒越狱评测应以目标模型调用预算为核心约束，而非仅报告孤立ASR或难以获取的FLOPs。Fair-ASR揭示了现有比较中的预算混淆问题，并表明低成本策略在受限访问场景中仍有重要价值。ReCode验证了组合式设计可显著改善双重调用效率，但摘要中的结果主要覆盖特定预算和模型，跨模型、跨任务及更严格防护条件下的泛化能力仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Reliable jailbreak evaluation is essential for assessing LLM safety, but most existing studies rely solely on attack success rate (ASR) without accounting for its dependence on attack budgets, resulting in unfair comparisons across methods. Existing compute-aware evaluations reduce heterogeneous resources into FLOPs, which is difficult to estimate for black-box models and fails to capture resource-specific constraints. To provide a comparable evaluation basis, we introduce Fair-ASR, an evaluation protocol for black-box jailbreak attacks under shared target-call budgets B, using target calls as a directly observable and method-agnostic comparison axis while tracking attacker calls separately for efficiency analysis. We re-evaluate 11 representative attacks under the Fair-ASR protocol and find that attack rankings change substantially across target-call budgets, simple stochastic perturbations and hand-crafted templates remain highly competitive under equal target access, and no evaluated LLM-driven method is efficient in both target and attacker calls. Motivated by this efficiency gap, we introduce ReCode, a compositional budget-efficient attack that combines desensitization rewriting with two effective low-cost primitives identified by Fair-ASR. Under a budget of 20 target calls, ReCode achieves 85% ASR on GPT-5 while requiring only 7.19 attacker calls per request on average, showing strong efficiency in both target and attacker calls.

</details>


### [104] [Trusted Workflow Relays:Cross-Tenant Email Abuse and Composable Red Team Initial-Access Primitives in Multi-Tenant Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17361)
*Priyank Nigam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“可信工作流中继”概念：云服务以受信任的提供商身份发送通知时，若应用层未正确约束发送者、收件人和消息内容，已认证用户仍可能跨租户诱导服务发送表面可信但未经授权的邮件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统邮件认证机制主要证明邮件来源域的真实性，无法证明某次应用内发送请求是否具备授权。多租户通知系统将参数提供者与实际发件服务主体分离，因而需要识别和系统化评估这一应用层授权缺口。

Method: 作者基于三个已负责任披露并修复的跨租户通知工作流案例进行比较分析：检查收件人租户绑定、后端与前端输入约束一致性、HTML/CSS净化、对象级访问控制、动作授权及令牌受众校验；并给出通知管线测试矩阵和与相关安全技术框架的映射。

Result: 第一个案例中，后端可绕过界面长度限制，原始HTML/CSS及链接可进入邮件，且CSS可隐藏服务生成文本；部分高风险嵌入内容和非Web协议被拦截。第二个案例同时存在收件人租户校验缺失及主题、HTML字段可控。第三个审批应用还叠加了访问控制薄弱、顺序对象标识符、动作授权缺失和令牌校验不完整等问题。论文指出，SPF、DKIM和DMARC即使验证通过，也不能证明应用层发送行为已获授权。

Conclusion: 该问题可视为传统开放邮件转发问题在应用层的演化：发件服务本身合法、请求者也已认证，但授权谓词未能保证“谁可让服务向谁发送何种内容”。缓解措施应包括严格的租户绑定、类型化模板与内容净化、对象级及动作级授权、令牌受众验证，以及可关联发送请求、身份和收件人的遥测审计。

Abstract: Cloud applications routinely send notifications through provider-operated mail identities, which improves deliverability but separates the actor who supplies notification parameters from the service principal that originates the message. In three responsibly disclosed and remediated cross-tenant notification workflows, an authenticated actor could reach recipients across tenant boundaries and, to varying degrees, control content that a trusted provider service delivered. In the first, backend requests bypassed a UI length limit, raw HTML and CSS survived into the delivered message, attacker links rendered, and CSS could hide service-controlled text; iframes and non-web URI schemes were rejected. The second combined missing recipient-tenant validation with attacker-controlled subject and HTML fields. The third, an approval application, added weak access control, sequential object identifiers, missing action authorization, and incomplete token validation, composing notification abuse with authorization failures.
  The pattern is analogous to a classical unauthenticated SMTP open relay, but the failure has moved up the stack: the actor is authenticated and the provider is the legitimate sender, yet application-layer authorization still fails to constrain who may cause it to send what to whom. We define a trusted workflow relay as a delivered, service-authentic message for which the application-level send-authorization predicate is false. We give a test matrix for notification pipelines, map the primitive to MITRE ATT&CK techniques for attachment-free phishing, and link it to device-code phishing (RFC 8628). SPF, DKIM, and DMARC can authenticate a message yet cannot establish that an application-level send was authorized. We conclude with controls for tenant binding, typed templates, object-level authorization, token audience validation, and identity telemetry.

</details>


### [105] [FESC: Remodeling Long-Context Private Inference with Encrypted State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17442)
*Yufan Zhu,Chao Jin,Khin Mi Mi Aung,Xiaokui Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出 Factorized Encrypted Scan-Contract（FESC），一种结合全同态加密（FHE）与安全多方计算（MPC）的私有长上下文选择性状态空间模型（SSM）推理框架。该方法面向 Mamba-2 等模型，在单张 A100 GPU 上实现了长度至少为 1,024 的端到端私密推理，并在长度 2,048、12 层 Mamba-base 配置下以 77.3 分钟完成推理、峰值显存为 32.7 GB，同时保持接近明文的任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有私密 Transformer 推理虽可优化加密注意力计算，但注意力的二次 token 对计算会随序列增长而成为主要瓶颈。SSM 具有线性时间递推优势，却难以直接适配加密计算：朴素方案可能导致乘法深度线性增长、需长期保存全序列状态，或引入高代价的稠密 FHE-MPC 数据转换。因此，需要一种能保留选择性 SSM 线性扩展性、同时降低混合加密协议转换与状态管理成本的私密推理机制。

Method: FESC 采用混合 FHE-MPC 架构，并设计因子化 scan-contract 算法。其核心是将输入相关的状态转移以紧凑因子形式跨越 FHE-MPC 转换边界，避免将其稠密展开；通过组合这些紧凑转移完成扫描计算，按需流式处理状态块以降低状态驻留开销，并在转换前完成输出收缩。针对 Mamba-2，作者实现了 GPU 优化的 CKKS 线性计算内核，并使用 MPC 执行 SiLU、softplus、指数和 RMSNorm 等非线性操作；同时采用考虑近似误差的微调，以减轻加密数值近似对模型精度的影响。

Result: 该实现展示了 scan-contract 对不变型与选择性 SSM 架构的接口兼容性。实验表明，FESC 可在单 GPU 上完成原生端到端、序列长度不低于 1,024 的私密长文档推理。对于长度 2,048 的输入，12 层 Mamba-base 在单张 A100 上的推理耗时为 77.3 分钟，峰值内存占用为 32.7 GB；在所评测的长文档任务中，准确率接近对应明文执行结果。

Conclusion: FESC 表明，利用因子化状态扫描与按需状态流式处理，可将选择性 SSM 的线性递推结构有效迁移至 FHE-MPC 私密推理场景，并避免稠密状态转移表示带来的主要成本。其贡献在于为长上下文私密推理提供了区别于加密 Transformer 注意力的可扩展路线；但从报告的 77.3 分钟延迟和 32.7 GB 内存看，系统更适合高隐私需求下的离线或低频推理，距离通用实时部署仍需进一步优化。

Abstract: Processing long, sensitive documents with machine-learning models requires efficient, privacy-preserving long-context inference. Prior private inference systems optimize or distribute encrypted Transformer attention, but its quadratic token-pair work remains the bottleneck as sequence length grows. Selective state-space models (SSMs) offer linear-time recurrence, yet direct encrypted implementation incurs linear multiplicative depth, sequence-wide state residency, or dense FHE-MPC conversion. We present Factorized Encrypted Scan-Contract (FESC), a hybrid FHE-MPC system for private long-context selective SSM inference. Its factorized scan-contract keeps input-dependent transitions compact across conversion boundaries, composes them without dense expansion, streams state chunks on demand, and contracts outputs before conversion. We demonstrate interface compatibility of the scan-contract implementation across invariant and selective SSM architectures. For our Mamba-2 instantiation, we design GPU-optimized CKKS kernels for linear computations, MPC protocols for SiLU, softplus, exponential, and RMSNorm, with approximation-aware fine-tuning. To our knowledge, FESC is the first private long-document inference system to complete native end-to-end execution at $L \geq 1{,}024$ on a single GPU. At $L = 2{,}048$, a 12-layer Mamba-base model completes inference in 77.3 minutes on one A100 GPU with a peak memory footprint of 32.7 GB, while maintaining near-plaintext accuracy on the evaluated long-document tasks.

</details>


### [106] [Decomposition Attacks Across Unlinkable Identities: Limits of Stateful Defenses for LLM Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17445)
*Bowen Sun,Zhengyue Zhao,Xiaogeng Liu,Yinzhi Cao,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究针对大语言模型“任务分解攻击”的有状态防御可行性。结论是：在攻击者可使用不可关联身份、并在外部整合答案的条件下，仅依赖请求分组的防御存在根本局限；若攻击者还能依据允许/拦截反馈重试，则实用的安全—效用平衡基本消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务多采用逐请求、无状态的安全判断。攻击者可将原本应被拒绝的目标拆成多个表面合规的请求，再组合回复完成目标。论文关注的核心问题是：当缺乏可靠身份关联信号时，有状态监控能否有效识别并阻断此类攻击，同时维持对正常用户的低误拒率。

Method: 论文先对固定且不重试的攻击策略建立理论刻画，证明安全性与效用的可达边界完全由具有相同能力需求的正常请求如何被分组决定；随后分析攻击者可根据Allow/Block反馈进行重试的情形。实验部分使用91个可执行任务及11,393条能力匹配的正常请求，比较10种策略，其中包括拥有精确“请求—操作”映射的特权策略，并设定能力匹配请求1%、无关背景流量0.5%的拒绝上限。

Result: 理论上，若正常请求存在持久且可识别的分组，可形成一定有效防御；若分组不断更新且与攻击请求不可区分，则无法获得有用防御。允许重试时，判定反馈会泄露哪些请求可通过，却不能表明拦截是否正确，从而破坏此前的可用工作点。实验显示，在给定误拒预算下，10种测试策略均无法同时阻止攻击并满足预算；面对防御阶段未见的任务族，攻击首轮成功率至少为99%，两轮后达到100%。

Conclusion: 单靠跨请求状态与内容分组，无法在弱身份关联环境中可靠抵御任务分解攻击。有效防御需要引入与分组直接相关的额外证据或约束，例如可信身份关联、提高新身份创建或使用成本，以及对答案后续使用环节施加控制。

Abstract: Most large language model services use stateless defenses, which judge only the current request, to refuse harmful tasks. Decomposition attacks exploit this limitation by splitting a harmful task into individually permissible requests and combining their answers. Defending against them therefore requires a stateful monitor that considers requests together. If it can group all requests for one attacker task, it can stop the attack. However, attackers can use unlinkable identities and combine answers elsewhere, leaving no reliable grouping signal. We ask whether decomposition attacks can still be stopped under this setting. For a fixed attack strategy without retries, we prove that the achievable security and utility tradeoff depends entirely on how benign requests for the same capabilities are grouped. Persistent, recognizable groups permit a useful defense; fresh, indistinguishable groups do not. When attackers can retry and learn from Allow/Block decisions, this useful operating point disappears: the feedback reveals what passes but not whether a block was correct. Experiments on 91 executable tasks and 11,393 capability-matched benign requests support these results. Under a 1% denial cap for these requests and a 0.5% cap for unrelated background traffic, all ten tested policies, including one privileged policy with an exact request-to-operation map, either fail to stop attacks or exceed the budget. On defense-unseen task families, attack success is at least 99% after one attempt and 100% after two. Effective defenses therefore require additional evidence or mechanisms tied to grouping, such as reliable identity linkage, costs for fresh identities, or control over answer use.

</details>


### [107] [KeyPooling: Measuring Where LLM API Relay Paths Collapse Prompt Cache Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17485)
*Bowen Sun,Yixi Cai,Xiaogeng Liu,Zhengyue Zhao,Yinzhi Cao,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示：LLM API 中继若让多个客户共用上游凭证，且未在最终提示缓存查询与写入前保留客户级身份或命名空间隔离，则不同客户可能读取彼此的缓存状态，并在特定条件下实现逐令牌恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作虽已观察到部分端点存在缓存共享，但尚未厘清究竟是上游凭证、凭证池、适配器还是嵌套中继决定最终缓存身份。该研究旨在系统定位身份转换链路，并评估共享凭证架构下的跨客户缓存泄露范围与缓解成本。

Method: 作者提出 KeyPooling 测量方法：分别追踪客户身份在缓存查找和写入阶段的传递，验证运行时身份变换，并按单一预测身份组件逐项构造对照实验。实验覆盖五个连接 OpenAI 与 Anthropic 的开源网关；同时使用按周、与结果无关的 OpenRouter 抽样框架评估实际覆盖面，并通过受控恢复流程验证连续目标位置恢复能力。

Result: 五个网关默认均未将客户绑定到独立上游凭证；在共享凭证条件下，全部网关均对两家提供方暴露跨客户缓存读取。实验进一步将决定性因素定位到主体与命名空间切分、凭证池关联、适配器以及嵌套中继变换。在 OpenRouter 测试中，覆盖了符合条件令牌量的80.5%，28个标签中有12个出现跨账户读取，占流量33.7%。某条生产路由上，受控实验恢复了连续8个目标位置。缓存粒度、路由、速率限制、归因和预算会影响恢复是否可行，但不能构成可靠的隔离机制。

Conclusion: 论文提出的防御契约是：每位客户必须进入由提供方强制执行的独立域，或者由经认证身份派生的命名空间必须贯穿所有最终缓存查询与写入。将隔离边界置于可复用公共前缀之后，可在保留大部分缓存复用收益的同时，仅带来约1.7%至2.5%的建模成本上升。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) API relays authenticate customers separately but often forward requests through shared provider credentials. Providers scope prompt caches to upstream principals and namespaces, so relay customers mapped to one cache identity can observe each other's cache state. Prior work showed cache sharing at selected endpoints but did not identify which credential, pool, adapter, or nested hop controls the finalidentity. We present KeyPooling, a measurement method that traces customer identity through cache lookup and write, verifies runtime transformations, and tests one predicted identity component at a time. Across five open-source gateways connected to OpenAI and Anthropic, none bound customers to upstream credentials by default; under a shared credential, all five exposed cross-customer cache reads for both providers. Principal and namespace splits, pool associations, and adapter and nested-relay contrasts localized the controlling transformations. In an outcome-independent weekly OpenRouter frame, tests covered 80.5% of eligible token volume and found cross-account reads for 12 of 28 labels carrying 33.7% of volume. On one production route, a controlled procedure recovered eight consecutive target positions without target access. Broader tests identify cache granularity, routing, rate limits, attribution, and budget as conditions for token-by-token recovery, not security controls. We derive a defense contract: every customer must enter a provider-enforced domain, or a namespace derived from authenticated identity must survive every final cache lookup and write. Placing this split after reusable public prefixes preserved most modeled reuse at a 1.7-2.5% cost increase.

</details>


### [108] [Cross-Domain Joint DDoS Detection in Multi-Controller SDN via Confidence-Based Entropy Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17507)
*Zhaoyang Zhang,Shen Wang,Xiaofeng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文针对多控制器SDN中跨域DDoS检测的聚合偏差问题，提出跨域置信度融合框架；在不共享原始流量的条件下，显著降低聚合控制器误报率并提升F1值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于熵的检测器在单控制器场景有效，但直接迁移至多控制器环境时，跨域流量呈现源端分散、目标端汇聚特征。攻击结束后的过渡阶段，边缘控制器已恢复正常而聚合控制器仍持续误报，造成检测结果不一致和告警质量下降。

Method: 作者通过系统实验定位聚合偏差，并将其归因于OpenFlow统计信息滞后与无约束动态阈值漂移的耦合作用。随后设计跨域置信度融合机制：边缘控制器仅发送轻量级置信信息，用于校准聚合控制器的判定，无需传输原始流量数据；该方案强调非侵入、低通信开销与渐进式部署。

Result: 在包含3个控制器、24台主机的线性Mininet测试环境中，经过10轮实验，所提方法保持边缘控制器检测性能，同时将聚合控制器误报率由8.87%降至1.96%，F1值由89.04%提升至96.89%。

Conclusion: 跨域置信度融合能够有效缓解多控制器SDN中攻击后过渡阶段的聚合误报问题。其核心价值在于以有限控制面信息实现跨域协同校准，兼顾检测精度、通信效率和实际部署可行性；但摘要未说明不同拓扑规模、攻击强度及统计延迟条件下的泛化表现。

Abstract: In multi-controller Software-Defined Networking (SDN), Distributed Denial-of-Service (DDoS) attacks exhibit a "dispersed source, concentrated target" pattern across domains, i.e., attack traffic originates from multiple edge-controller domains but converges on a victim in a single aggregation controller domain.
